### [2] [Solomon zeta functions over arithmetic orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12302)
*Sean B. Lynch*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了非阿基米德局部域上有限维半单代数的序上格点的Solomon第一猜想的一个有效版本，将部分Solomon zeta函数与对应最大序zeta函数的商表示为有限和，其项由有限模论数据决定，并由有限子模偏序集的Möbius函数定义的多项式加权。该表达式不依赖于所选的最大超序，证明纯代数且首先针对精细Bushnell-Reiner zeta函数提出，并应用于$\mathbb{Z}_p[\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}]$上所有格点的Solomon zeta函数显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solomon猜想是代数数论和表示论中的重要问题，涉及局部域上格点的zeta函数结构。有效版本的证明有助于理解模论数据与zeta函数之间的深层联系，并为非投射格点提供显式计算工具。

Method: 采用纯代数方法，将部分Solomon zeta函数与最大序zeta函数的商转化为有限和，利用Möbius函数对有限子模偏序集进行加权，并首先针对精细Bushnell-Reiner zeta函数建立该表达式，最后推广到Solomon zeta函数。

Result: 得到了Solomon第一猜想的有效版本，即上述商可表示为与最大超序选择无关的有限和形式，并给出了$\mathbb{Z}_p[\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}]$上所有格点（包括非投射格点）的Solomon zeta函数显式公式。

Conclusion: 成功证明了Solomon第一猜想的有效版本，建立了模论数据与zeta函数之间的显式联系，为更一般情形下的计算和应用提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove an effective version of Solomon's first conjecture for lattices over orders in finite-dimensional semisimple algebras over nonarchimedean local fields. We express the quotient of a partial Solomon zeta function by the corresponding maximal-order zeta function as a finite sum whose terms are determined by finite module-theoretic data and weighted by polynomials defined using the Möbius function of finite submodule posets. The resulting expression is independent of the chosen maximal overorder. Our proof is purely algebraic and is first formulated for the refined Bushnell--Reiner zeta functions. As an application, we obtain explicit formulas for the Solomon zeta functions of all lattices over $\mathbb{Z}_p[\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}]$, including non-projective lattices.

</details>

### [4] [Odd Parts of Derivative Period Polynomials and a Logarithmic Transition Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12378)
*Seokho Jin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了偶数权重的规范化水平一Hecke本征形的奇周期多项式$Q^-_{f,m}$的非零零点要么在单位圆上，要么构成一个实倒数四元组$\{\pm b,\pm b^{-1}\}$（$0<b<1$），且该四元组仅出现在有限个权重-导数对中。当导数阶$m$足够大时，所有非零零点均为单根且位于单位圆上。文章还刻画了大权重下四元组出现的相变临界尺度$m_c(k)=(k-1)\log((k-1)/\pi)$，并给出了精确的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者此前已证明完整周期多项式$Q_{f,m}$的零点全部位于单位圆上，但取奇部后可能产生离圆零点，因此需要系统研究$Q^-_{f,m}$的零点分布规律。

Method: 采用精确的奇自反逆补全方法、边界敏感的卷绕数计数技术，以及分裂Mellin积分的均匀鞍点估计。

Result: (1) 存在绝对常数$K_0$，使得对$k\ge K_0$的所有偶数权重，$Q^-_{f,m}$的非零零点要么在单位圆上，要么构成唯一一个实倒数四元组；(2) 当$m$足够大时所有非零零点为单位圆上的单根；(3) 当$m/m_c(k)\toθ\in(0,1)$时恰出现一个四元组，内正零点趋于$(1+θ)/2$；当$θ>1$时所有零点在单位圆上；(4) 临界情形$θ=1$时结论同样成立。

Conclusion: 奇周期多项式的零点分布完全由权重与导数阶的相对大小决定，任何脱离单位圆/实轴的现象仅出现在有限个参数对中，大权重下存在清晰的相变行为。

Abstract: Let $f$ be a normalized level-one Hecke eigenform of even weight $k$, and let $Q_{f,m}$ be the period polynomial formed from the critical values of the $m$-th derivative of its completed $L$-function. We study the odd part $Q^-_{f,m}(z)=(Q_{f,m}(z)-Q_{f,m}(-z))/2$, retaining the zero at the origin forced by oddness. A unit-circle theorem for the full polynomial does not settle this problem: taking an odd part can create off-circle zeros even when the original polynomial has all of its zeros on the unit circle.
  We prove that there is an absolute $K_0$ such that, for every even $k\ge K_0$, every normalized level-one Hecke eigenform $f$ of weight $k$, and every integer $m\ge0$, the nonzero zeros of $Q^-_{f,m}$ off the unit circle, if any, form a single real reciprocal quartet $\{\pm b,\pm b^{-1}\}$ with $0<b<1$. For each fixed weight, all nonzero zeros are simple and lie on the unit circle once $m$ is sufficiently large. Hence any failure of the real-or-unit-circle containment is confined to finitely many weight--derivative pairs.
  We also determine the large-weight transition of the possible quartet. Its critical scale is $m_c(k)=(k-1)\log((k-1)/π)$. If $m/m_c(k)\toθ\in(0,1)$, exactly one quartet occurs and its inner positive zero tends to $(1+θ)/2$; if $θ>1$, every nonzero zero is simple and lies on the unit circle. At the critical ratio $θ=1$, the same real-or-unit-circle containment remains valid. More precisely, if $|m-m_c(k)|/\log k\to\infty$, the sign of $m-m_c(k)$ determines the phase. We also obtain first-order formulas for the quartet on the resolved pre-critical side and for positive derivative orders $m=O(\log k)$. The proof combines an exact odd self-inversive completion, a boundary-sensitive winding count, and uniform saddle estimates for a split Mellin integral.

</details>


### [5] [Local statistics and average rank of genus $g$ hyperelliptic curves with a Weierstrass point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12381)
*Keunyoung Jeong,Junyeong Park*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了数域上带Weierstrass点的亏格g超椭圆曲线在剩余特征大于2g+1的给定素点处具有好约化的概率，并得到了包括正环面秩或幂单秩情形在内的其他约化类型的类似概率公式。作为应用，在假设Hasse-Weil猜想和广义黎曼假设下，推导了带Weierstrass点的亏格g超椭圆曲线平均解析秩的显式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数域上超椭圆曲线的约化类型概率分布，并利用这些概率结果推导其平均解析秩的上界。

Method: 通过数论与代数几何方法，计算带Weierstrass点的亏格g超椭圆曲线在不同约化类型（好约化、正环面秩、正幂单秩等）下的概率，并基于Hasse-Weil猜想和广义黎曼假设分析平均解析秩。

Result: 获得了超椭圆曲线好约化及其他约化类型的精确概率公式，并在标准解析假设下得到了平均解析秩的显式上界。

Conclusion: 该工作为超椭圆曲线约化类型的概率分布提供了完整刻画，并在标准数论猜想下给出了平均解析秩的约束，深化了对超椭圆曲线算术性质的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we determine the probability that a genus $g$ hyperelliptic curve with a Weierstrass point over a number field has good reduction at a given prime of residue characteristic $>2g+1$. We also obtain analogous probability formulas for several other reduction types, including cases with positive toric or unipotent rank. As an application, assuming the Hasse--Weil conjecture and the generalized Riemann hypothesis, we derive an explicit upper bound for the average analytic rank of genus $g$ hyperelliptic curves with a Weierstrass point.

</details>

### [7] [Euler systems and the symmetric square of a Hida family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12679)
*Debanjana Kundu,Jishnu Ray,Stefano Vigni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对素数 p≥7，为 p-adic Hida 族的对称平方构造了一个非平凡的 Euler 系统（推广了 Loeffler-Zerbes 对 p-ordinary 新形式的对称平方 Euler 系统），并证明了在该设定下对偶 Selmer 群的代数函数方程，最后基于 Büyükboduk-Ganguly 在代数 p-adic L-函数函数方程方面的工作，证明了 Hida 族对称平方的 Iwasawa 主猜想的一个可除性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Loeffler-Zerbes 为 p-ordinary 新形式的对称平方构造的 Euler 系统推广到 Hida 族（即 p-adic 族）的对称平方情形，并进一步建立 Iwasawa 主猜想的相关结果。

Method: 通过构造非平凡的 Euler 系统，证明对偶 Selmer 群的代数函数方程，并利用 Büyükboduk-Ganguly 关于代数 p-adic L-函数函数方程的最新工作，推导主猜想方向的可除性结论。

Result: 成功构造了 Hida 族对称平方的非平凡 Euler 系统；建立了对偶 Selmer 群的代数函数方程；证明了 Hida 族对称平方的 Iwasawa 主猜想的一个可除性结果。

Conclusion: 本文推进了 Hida 族对称平方的 Iwasawa 理论研究，为后续建立完整的主猜想提供了关键工具和部分结果。

Abstract: Let $p\geq7$ be a prime number. We build a non-trivial Euler system for the symmetric square of a $p$-adic Hida family of modular forms interpolating the Euler system constructed by Loeffler-Zerbes for the symmetric square of a $p$-ordinary newform. As a second contribution, we prove an algebraic functional equation for dual Selmer groups in this setting. Finally, building on recent work by Büyükboduk-Ganguly on functional equations of algebraic (Rankin-Selberg) $p$-adic $L$-functions, we prove a divisibility result towards the Iwasawa main conjecture for the symmetric square of a Hida family.

</details>


### [8] [Cyclic Projective Orbits on Rational Normal Curves and MDS Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12761)
*Yangcheng Li,Pingzhi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环算子与Krylov码的MDS轨道段与有理正规曲线的关系，给出了广义Reed-Solomon（GRS）系数的完整分类，证明了非GRS的MDS同伴码在有限域上随域大小趋于无穷时占比趋近于1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解循环算子作用下MDS轨道段的几何结构，分类GRS码在同伴算子下的所有可能情形，并刻画GRS系数轨迹的代数几何性质。

Method: 利用PGL₂的(r-1)次对称幂作用将MDS轨道段与有理正规曲线相联系；在代数闭域上研究GRS系数轨迹的Zariski闭包，通过二维环面参数化并考虑反转商；利用Frobenius下降给出精确计数公式；通过典范余子校验矩阵刻画MDS轨迹的开条件。

Result: 对于r≥3, n≥r+3，MDS轨道段位于有理正规曲线上当且仅当(A,[z])共轭于PGL₂对称幂作用；伴随算子GRS系数分为分裂半单、两种非分裂半单和单根族四类；GRS系数轨迹的Zariski闭包是秩为r-2的不可约有理曲面；给出了F_q上GRS多项式的精确计数；固定r,n，F_q上同伴码为MDS且非GRS的比例当q→∞时趋于1。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了循环算子框架下GRS码的代数结构，揭示了大多数MDS码在有限域上并非GRS码的渐近行为。

Abstract: Let \(A\) be a cyclic operator on an \(r\)-dimensional vector space over a field \(k\), and let \(z\) be a cyclic vector. Their Krylov code has parity-check matrix \((z,Az,\ldots,A^{n-1}z)\). For \(r\ge 3\) and \(n\ge r+3\), we prove that an MDS orbit segment lies on a rational normal curve precisely when the projective pair \((A,[z])\) is conjugate to one arising from the \((r-1)\)-st symmetric-power action of \(\mathrm{PGL}_2\). Over finite fields, for companion operators, this gives a complete classification of the generalized Reed--Solomon locus into split semisimple, two nonsplit semisimple, and unipotent families.
  Over an algebraically closed field \(k\), the Zariski closure \(\GRSsurf_{r,k}\) of the semisimple GRS coefficient locus is an irreducible rational surface, generically parameterized two-to-one by a two-dimensional torus of geometric-progression root sets; reversal is the generic ambiguity. The affine quotient of the parameter torus by reversal is the normalization of \(\GRSsurf_{r,k}\cap D(a_0)\), its nonzero-constant-term open part. The codimension in the space of monic degree-\(r\) polynomials is \(r-2\). Frobenius descent gives an exact formula for the number of GRS polynomials over \(\mathbb F_q\). A canonical remainder parity-check matrix defines the MDS locus by a principal open condition. For fixed \(r\ge3\) and \(n\ge r+3\), the proportion of all monic degree-\(r\) polynomials over \(\mathbb F_q\) whose companion codes are MDS and non-GRS tends to one as \(q\to\infty\) through prime powers.

</details>

Abstract: For any irrational number $ α>\frac{3+\sqrt{5}}{2}\approx2.618$ and given a positive $ n\in\mathbb{N} $, we use elementary number theory to introduce a necessary and sufficient condition for a natural number $ x $ to be in the $n$th iterate of the Beatty sequence of modulus $α$.

### [10] [Ranks of Elliptic Curves Twisted by Quadratic Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13000)
*Mohammad H. Hamdar,Cihan Sabuncu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了存在无穷多个可以表示为两平方数之和的二次扭曲d，使得椭圆曲线E^d的秩为1。该结果通过模L-函数导数的矩方法实现，特别捕捉了Munshi工作中遗漏的低阶导数，并给出了椭圆纤维化(1+t²)y²=f(x)的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究椭圆曲线二次扭曲的秩分布问题，特别是弥补Munshi工作中未能处理低阶导数的缺陷，并探索与椭圆纤维化相关的数论结构。

Method: 利用模L-函数导数的矩方法（moments of derivatives of modular L-functions），通过分析低阶导数来构造具有秩1的无穷多个二次扭曲。

Result: 证明存在无穷多个可表示为两平方数之和的二次扭曲d，使得椭圆曲线E^d的秩为1。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅推进了椭圆曲线二次扭曲秩分布的理论，还揭示了椭圆纤维化(1+t²)y²=f(x)的相关数论性质。

Abstract: Let $E$ be an elliptic curve over $\mathbb{Q}$ and let $E^d$ be its twist by the quadratic character $χ_d$. We prove there are infinitely many twists $d$ which are sums of two squares such that $E^d$ has rank $1$. This result is achieved using moments of derivatives of modular $L$-functions, and particularly captures the lower derivatives which were left out in the work of Munshi. Such a result, in particular, also gives us information on the elliptic fibration $(1+t^2)y^2=f(x)$, where $f(x)$ is a cubic polynomial.

</details>

### [11] [Expansion of the planetary Hamiltonian for small eccentricities and inclinations: a vector formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12003)
*Federico Mogavero*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于向量形式重新推导了小偏心率和小相互倾角条件下的行星哈密顿量展开式，证明了扰动函数的长期部分和所有傅里叶谐波均可表示为系统不变平面内向量的标量积，并展示了如何利用角动量和偏心向量来表达这些项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的行星哈密顿量展开通常基于复杂的标量角度变量，推导繁琐且物理图像不清晰。作者希望利用向量形式（角动量向量和偏心向量）重新推导，以获得更简洁、几何直观性更强的表达，从而简化理论分析并揭示其内在结构。

Method: 采用完全的向量形式（而非传统的轨道根数角度变量）重新推导行星哈密顿量的展开式；聚焦于小偏心率和小相互倾角情形；将扰动函数的长期部分和各傅里叶谐波表示为系统不变平面内向量的标量积。

Result: 成功推导出扰动函数的长期部分和所有傅里叶谐波均可被写成系统不变平面内向量（角动量向量和偏心向量）的标量积形式。

Conclusion: 向量形式提供了一种更简洁且几何直观的方式来描述行星摄动问题，有助于简化理论分析并可能为后续研究（如长期共振、潮汐演化等）提供更高效的工具。

Abstract: We revisit the classical expansion of the planetary Hamiltonian for small eccentricities and mutual inclinations of the orbits, presenting a derivation based entirely on vector formalism. We demonstrate that the secular part of the disturbing function, as well as any other Fourier harmonic, can be expressed in terms of scalar products of vectors lying in the system's invariant plane. Furthermore, we show how to express any such term using the angular momentum and eccentricity vectors of the orbits.

Abstract: In this paper, we show that for Schrödinger operators with weakly correlated ground states, the Hohenberg-Kohn theorem holds within the maximal class of form-bounded external potentials if and only if the single-particle density is positive quasi-everywhere. Abstract: History-dependent entropic formulations require a precise meaning for channelwise terminal variation and a spatial power split that remains valid for weak diffusion fields. Abstract: The North American Nanohertz Observatory for Gravitational Waves (NANOGrav) achieves sub-microsecond timing precision for several millisecond pulsars in its pulsar timing array (PTA) with the objective of detecting and characterizing nanohertz gravitational waves. PSR J1713+0747 is one of the most precisely timed pulsars in the array, achieving sub-microsecond timing precision. However, in April 2021, PSR J1713+0747 underwent a sudden and unusual change in pulse shape that disrupted its timing stability. As PSR J1713+0747 is a key contributor to PTA sensitivity, variations in its pulse profile significantly affect the array's sensitivity to nanohertz gravitational waves. We apply frequency-dependent Gaussian component models to decompose the pulse profile and track the evolution of individual components through the event. This component-level method maintains phase-connected timing across the shape-change event. At L-band, the recovered TOAs have a median uncertainty of ~0.47 microseconds compared to ~0.69 microseconds for standard template matching. At 820 MHz, where profile evolution is stronger, the recovered TOAs have a median uncertainty of ~1.63 microseconds compared to ~0.67 microseconds for standard template matching. The recovered TOAs achieve timing uncertainties comparable to conventional template matching while allowing data affected by profile variability to be retained in PTA gravitational-wave analyses. These results represent an initial step toward profile-domain timing methods capable of accounting for pulse-profile evolution while reducing the need for additional timing model parameters.

Abstract: Additional power sources to traditional supernovae (SNe) are necessary to account for the extreme luminosities of superluminous SNe (SLSNe). A main power source for hydrogen-rich SLSNe (SLSNe-II) is thought to be circumstellar material (CSM) interaction. However, the nature of underlying SNe and their progenitor systems remain elusive as they are hidden below strong CSM signatures. Here, we present optical photometry and spectroscopy of SLSNe-II 2017fck and 2019cmv. They are characterized by post-maximum "superlinear" light curves which we also identify in a sample of Type Ia SNe interacting with CSM (SNe Ia-CSM), along with their light-curve correlations and spectral similarities. Thus, we compute a numerical light-curve model grid of SNe Ia-CSM with various SN Ia subtypes and CSM distributions. Our model grid spans the observed parameter space of SNe Ia-CSM in terms of their rise times, peak luminosities, and decline rates with a wide CSM mass range of $\sim2-11$ M$_\odot$, indicating the diversity in their progenitor systems. For SNe 2017fck and 2019cmv, we infer high CSM masses of $\sim11$ and $6$ M$_\odot$, respectively, which might be produced during the common envelope evolution of a white dwarf and massive ($\geq8$ M$_\odot$) or intermediate-mass ($<8$ M$_\odot$) companion. Together with the proposed connection of SLSN-II 2006gy to SNe Ia-CSM, SNe 2017fck and 2019cmv may offer a possible thermonuclear origin for superlinear SLSNe-II with peak optical luminosities up to $10^{44}$ erg s$^{-1}$.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"磁驱动星震"模型来解释脉冲星自转突变（glitch）和反突变（anti-glitch）现象。与传统认为由自引力导致的星震不同，该研究假设磁应力引起的弹性变形触发了星震，这既可以增加也可以减小转动惯量，从而自然解释正负两种频率突变。通过量级估算，得到了磁场强度B与突变幅度Δν/ν之间的简单关系，与现有观测数据一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统星震模型假设脉冲星自转突变源于自引力导致的断层滑动，这只能产生正的频率突变（即自转加速）。然而，越来越多的磁星（强磁化脉冲星）被观测到出现反突变（自转减速），传统框架无法解释这一现象。本研究旨在提出一种新的机制来解释这两种突变。

Result: 得到了磁场B与频率突变幅度Δν/ν之间的简单定量关系，该关系与已观测到的glitch和anti-glitch数据的分布相一致。

Conclusion: 磁驱动星震模型可以自然解释脉冲星的正反两种自转突变现象。未来需要更多glitch/anti-glitch事件的发现以及更定量的弹-磁应力耦合模型来进一步验证这一假设。

Abstract: In the conventional starquake model of pulsar glitches, it is usually assumed that such events arise from fault slip induced by the self-gravity of compact objects. This inevitably decreases the moment of inertia, producing a glitch with an amplitude of only $Δν/ν> 0$. However, an increasing number of anti-glitches ($Δν/ν< 0$) have been observed in extremely magnetized pulsars, the magnetars, and this cannot be explained by that framework. In the present study, we hypothesis that magnetic stresses within a compact object can make for elastic deformations that trigger fault slipping, resulting in a ``magnetism-driven starquake'' when the local breaking threshold is exceeded. This process can then either decrease or increase the moment of inertia, naturally generating a glitch or an anti-glitch, respectively. With an order-of-magnitude calculation in this brief report, we present a simple relationship between the magnetic field $B$ and the amplitude $Δν/ν$, which is consistent with the observational distribution of existing glitch and anti-glitch data. Further discoveries of glitch/anti-glitch events, alongside more quantitative models of elastic-magnetic stress coupling, would be welcome and could eventually provide clear tests for the hypothesis.

</details>

We demonstrate that the framework accurately reconstructs central EoS posteriors for NICER-like observations while preserving the mapping between macroscopic stellar observables and the microscopic properties of dense matter. Exploiting the computational efficiency of the cINN, we perform a systematic optimisation study of 62,400 simulated mass--radius observations to identify the most informative targets for constraining the high-density EoS. We find that the constraining power depends strongly on the location of the observation in the mass--radius plane, with an optimal strategy that alternates between compact high-mass stars and extended intermediate-mass stars, reducing the uncertainty in the inferred EoS by up to $\sim 9\%-10\%$ relative to the current NICER baseline. These results establish physics-informed invertible neural networks as a powerful framework for rapid, physically consistent inference of dense-matter properties from present and future multi-messenger observations.

Abstract: The origin of gamma-ray burst (GRB) prompt emission remains an open question. The internal shock (IS) model is a leading scenario for converting relativistic ejecta kinetic energy into gamma rays, but its parameters have not yet been fully calibrated against observed GRB light curves (LCs) to reproduce their diversity. We adopt a machine-learning framework to optimise the IS model by comparing simulated and observed LC properties from three GRB catalogues (Swift/BAT, Fermi/GBM, CGRO/BATSE). Assuming a redshift-dependent GRB formation rate, we employ a genetic algorithm to minimise a loss function based on six independent metrics capturing both average behaviours and statistical distributions. The optimised model reproduces several key observational properties, including the average post-peak temporal profile, autocorrelation function, and the distributions of duration, signal-to-noise ratio, number of peaks, peak flux, and fluence. We also derive constraints on the central engine activity: (i) the number of emitted shells is well described by a generalised Zipf distribution, analogous to the Gutenberg-Richter law for earthquakes, and (ii) the rest-frame shell-emission times follow a negative exponential distribution, indicating a stochastic process with a constant ejection probability. This calibrated IS model provides a physically grounded framework for interpreting GRB variability and predicting GRB populations detectable by future missions.

Abstract: Although symmetry-protected topological phases (SPTs) can be distinguished by their entanglement properties, it has been unclear how to extract this information directly from expectation values beyond the 1D case. Abstract: Topological materials, characterized by symmetry-protected nontrivial band structures such as Dirac cones and Weyl nodes, host diverse quantum phenomena, with potential applications in quantum transport, spintronics, and nonlinear optics. Ultrafast pump-probe spectroscopy has emerged as a powerful tool for exploring nonequilibrium dynamics in these systems. Its femtosecond resolution allows charge, spin, orbital, and lattice interactions to be tracked on their intrinsic timescales, thereby revealing key coupling mechanisms in topological phases. This review summarizes progress in ultrafast spectroscopic studies of topological insulators, topological semimetals, and magnetic topological materials. We first discuss the relaxation pathways of photoexcited surface and bulk electronic states, emphasizing electron-phonon scattering, surface-bulk charge transfer, and ultrafast spin conversion. We then examine population inversion in Dirac and Weyl semimetals, spin-polarization dynamics associated with tilted Weyl bands, and the effects of magnetic order on topological states, including coherent phonon and magnon excitations, magnetically driven topological transitions, and terahertz emission. We further review photoinduced topological phase transitions driven by electronic correlations, lattice distortions, and magnetic order under intense optical excitation, highlighting routes toward nonthermal control of quantum phases. Finally, we outline future directions that combine multidimensional ultrafast spectroscopy with temporal, energy, momentum, and spin resolution and advanced theoretical modeling to establish a unified picture of nonequilibrium topological states. This review aims to provide a useful reference for ultrafast studies of topological quantum materials and to advance their applications in high-speed, low-power information processing, spintronics, and quantum technologies.

Abstract: We construct an exact spin-$1$ parent Hamiltonian for constrained valence-bond loop manifolds on the checkerboard and pyrochlore lattices. The Hamiltonian is local, SU(2)- and time-reversal-invariant, and built from positive-semidefinite projectors acting on triangular faces. Each projector removes only the maximally polarized state of a triangle, so the model is frustration-free. Its zero-energy states are generated by an AKLT-like construction in which each spin-$1$ moment is resolved into two virtual spin-$\tfrac12$ degrees of freedom, singlets are formed inside every crossed plaquette or tetrahedron, and the physical spin-$1$ Hilbert space is recovered by projection. The resulting ground states are fully packed singlet-loop states on the corner-sharing lattice. Thus, a loop or dimer constraint, usually introduced as part of an effective Rokhsar--Kivelson description, appears here as the exact zero-energy manifold of a microscopic spin Hamiltonian. We analyze spin correlations within this manifold and show that, for a fixed loop covering, they are determined by loop connectivity. We also project symmetry-allowed perturbations into the ground-state manifold and derive the resulting low-energy pseudospin dynamics. The checkerboard and pyrochlore cases differ sharply. On the pyrochlore lattice, tetrahedral symmetry removes simple local bias terms, and the leading nontrivial next-nearest-neighbour Heisenberg perturbation gives an emergent spin-$\tfrac12$ XY model on the diamond lattice of tetrahedron centers. These results give an exact spin-$1$ microscopic starting point for constrained valence-bond physics in two and three dimensions, and show how loop, dimer, and gauge-theoretic descriptions can be approached from a small-spin, SU(2)-invariant frustrated magnet.

</details>

An example gives a new perspective on the old equivalence between simplicial sets and topological spaces: both are equivalent to simplicial topological spaces, and the equivalence implies a curiously close relationship between realizations of simplicial spaces and realizations of their underlying simplicial sets, viewed as discrete simplicial spaces.

Motivation: 口传民间旋律与蛋白质序列具有相似性（一维、有限字母表），但旋律中的节奏信息破坏了标准序列比对算法的假设，阻碍了生物信息学方法在文化进化研究中的应用。现有方法无法大规模自动化处理旋律对齐问题。

Method: 开发了一种节奏感知的对齐方法，应用于40000首爱尔兰舞曲变体，实现首个大规模自动化旋律对齐；随后运用四种经典生物信息学分析（突变性、替换矩阵、位置保守性、协方差）进行比较研究。

Result: 四种生物信息学分析揭示了旋律进化与分子进化截然不同的模式：蛋白质进化受生化与生物物理约束驱动，而旋律进化则受记忆、运动和社会偏见塑造。

Conclusion: 生物信息学为研究文化进化提供了概念性和算法性的强大框架，尽管音乐文化传播已被讨论数个世纪，但本文展示了如何在大尺度上对其进行定量分析。

Abstract: Evolved sequences can be used to infer the rules of evolution. Orally transmitted folk melodies are evolved sequences whose similarity to protein sequences (one-dimensional, drawn from a limited alphabet) invites application of bioinformatics methods to study cultural evolution. A major obstacle is that melodies encode rhythm, which breaks some assumptions of standard sequence-alignment algorithms. We develop a rhythm-aware alignment method and apply it to \num{40000} Irish dance tune variants, enabling the first large-scale automated melodic alignment. Four canonical bioinformatics analyses -- mutability, substitution matrices, positional conservation, and covariance -- reveal patterns distinct from those of molecular evolution, revealing the forces that shape each domain: biochemical and biophysical constraints for proteins; memory, motor, and social biases for melodies. Together the results show that bioinformatics provides a powerful framework -- conceptual as much as algorithmic -- for studying cultural evolution. Although the cultural transmission of music has been discussed for centuries, here we show how to analyze it at large scale.

</details>


### [53] [On Tree-Network Distinguishability and Full Identifiability of Phylogenetic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12919)
*Jari Brits,Niels Holtgrefe,Leo van Iersel,Samuel Martin*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Jukes-Cantor、Kimura 2-参数和Kimura 3-参数替换模型下，系统发育网络拓扑结构的可辨识性。结果表明，在合理的参数空间内，level-1系统发育网络的半有向网络参数是完全可以辨识的；此外，在JC和K2P模型下，除非网络是树或包含特定2-blob结构，否则网络与树无法诱导相同的叶模式分布，证明网状进化大多会产生可检测的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育网络相较于系统发育树能更全面地描述包含重组、水平基因转移和杂交等网状事件的进化历史，但其网络拓扑结构在马尔可夫替换模型下是否可辨识是一个基础且关键的问题。现有研究仅建立了通用可辨识性（除零测集外），缺乏在参数空间每一点的严格辨识性证明，且缺乏对网络与树能否产生相同叶模式分布这一根本性问题的回答。

Method: 论文基于Jukes-Cantor (JC)、Kimura 2-参数 (K2P)和Kimura 3-参数 (K3P)马尔可夫核苷酸替换模型，在替换率为概率性、混合参数非平凡（不为0或1）的合理参数空间上，利用代数方法分析level-1系统发育网络（模去三角形重定向）的叶模式分布，证明半有向网络参数的完全可辨识性；并通过对比网络与树的叶模式分布，证明除非网络退化为树或包含2-blob结构，否则二者分布必然不同。

Result: (1) 在JC、K2P和K3P模型下，level-1系统发育网络的半有向网络参数在整个参数空间上（而非仅零测集外）是完全可辨识的。(2) 在JC和K2P模型下，任何系统发育网络与树无法诱导相同的叶模式分布，除非该网络本身就是树或仅添加了某些称为2-blob的子结构，表明网状进化在大多数情况下在叶模式分布中留下了可检测的特征。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了系统发育网络拓扑在常见核苷酸替换模型下的严格可辨识性，为系统发育网络推断提供了理论基础；同时证明了网状进化与树状进化在叶模式分布上的可区分性，对理解网状进化的检测能力具有重要价值。这些结果还可推广到多种基于溯祖的模型，具有广泛的理论意义。

Abstract: Phylogenetic networks generalize phylogenetic trees to evolutionary histories that include reticulate events such as recombination, horizontal gene transfer, and hybridization. Under a Markov model of nucleotide substitution, a phylogenetic network determines a distribution of leaf-patterns. Here, we study the identifiability of the network topology from this distribution under the Jukes-Cantor (JC), Kimura 2-parameter (K2P), and Kimura 3-parameter (K3P) models. Our first result is that the semi-directed network parameter of a level-1 phylogenetic network (modulo redirecting triangles) is fully identifiable under all three models, on a biologically reasonable parameter space in which substitution rates are probabilistic and mixing parameters are non-trivial (i.e., not 0 or 1). In contrast to the generic identifiability established in prior work, this holds at every point of the parameter space, not merely off of a measure-zero subset. Our second result distinguishes phylogenetic networks from phylogenetic trees, on the same parameter space, under JC and K2P. We prove that no phylogenetic network and phylogenetic tree can induce the same leaf-pattern distribution unless the network is a tree, possibly augmented with certain substructures called $2$-blobs. This means the presence of reticulate evolution creates, in most cases, a detectable signature in the leaf-pattern distribution. More broadly, these results have consequences for identifiability beyond the models and network classes studied here, including for several coalescent-based models.

</details>

*Atsushi Katsuda*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 对于非交换幂零格点，经典Floquet-Bloch理论因非I型障碍无法适用。本文绕过该障碍，在有理Kirillov数据和有限维有理纤维上构建了精确的Bloch型替代理论，证明了不可约表示的限制定理，并在有限维有理纤维上构造了正有限可加Plancherel测度，恢复了离散Heisenberg情形下的Pytlik公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Floquet-Bloch理论仅适用于交换周期问题，对于非交换幂零格点，存在非I型障碍使得无法对全体酉对偶进行类似参数化。本文旨在绕过这一障碍，在表示论框架下为幂零格点构建Bloch型替代理论。

Method: 利用Malcev完备化将无挠有限生成幂零群Γ嵌入幂零李群G，通过有理Kirillov参数l∈g*_Q考察G的不可约酉表示π_l在Γ上的限制。该分支首先由有理极化诱导表示描述，在有限维表示相关的有理奇点处进一步分解为Γ的有限维不可约表示；并在这些有限维有理纤维上构造正有限可加Plancherel测度。

Result: 证明了G的不可约酉表示π_l限制到Γ的精确限制定理；在有限维有理纤维上建立了Fourier反演和正规化迹恒等式，并恢复了离散Heisenberg情形的Pytlik公式。

Conclusion: 本文为幂零非交换周期问题提供了不依赖完整酉对偶参数化的Bloch型替代方案，在表示论框架下推广了经典Floquet-Bloch理论，并在有限维有理纤维上建立了完备的调和分析结构。

Abstract: Classical Floquet--Bloch theory decomposes abelian periodic problems over the character torus of the lattice. For nonabelian nilpotent lattices, the non--type~I obstruction rules out a comparable parametrization of the full unitary dual. We do not attempt to remove this obstruction. Instead, we construct an exact Bloch-type replacement on the representation-theoretic part of the theory which is visible from rational Kirillov data and from finite-dimensional rational fibers.
  Let \(Γ\) be a torsion-free finitely generated nilpotent group and let \(G\) be its Malcev completion. For an irreducible unitary representation \(π_l\) of \(G\) attached to a rational Kirillov parameter \(l\in\mathfrak g_{\mathbb Q}^{*}\), we prove an exact restriction theorem for \(π_l|_Γ\). The branching is first described by induced representations attached to rational polarizations. On the rational odd locus relevant to finite-dimensional representations, it further decomposes into finite-dimensional irreducible representations of \(Γ\).
  On these finite-dimensional rational fibers we construct a positive finitely additive Plancherel measure. It gives Fourier inversion and normalized trace identities for nilpotent lattices, recovering Pytlik's formula in the discrete Heisenberg case.

</details>

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了$(C^\vee,C)$型双仿射Hecke代数的正、负两个稳定极限表示于几乎对称Laurent多项式空间上，通过研究Cherednik算子在$t$参数正负幂次重标度下的渐近行为，证明了极限算子的三角性，并构造了稳定极限Koornwinder多项式作为本征函数基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从$(C_n^\vee,C_n)$型有限秩DAHA的标准多项式表示出发，研究其在参数$t$的正负幂次重标度下的渐近极限行为，以构造稳定极限DAHA的表示。

Method: 采用Cherednik算子的正负幂次重标度方法，在几乎对称Laurent多项式环上定义极限算子；利用诱导Bruhat序证明极限算子的三角性；通过部分对称化算子作用于非对称Koornwinder多项式构造本征函数。

Conclusion: 成功构造了$(C^\vee,C)$型双仿射Hecke代数的两个稳定极限表示，并建立了相应的本征函数系统，为Koornwinder多项式理论提供了稳定极限视角。

Abstract: We construct two stable limit representations of the double affine Hecke algebra of type $(C^\vee,C)$ on the space of almost symmetric Laurent polynomials, namely the positive and negative stable limit representations. Starting from the standard polynomial representation of the finite rank DAHA of type $(C_n^\vee,C_n)$, we study the asymptotic behavior of the Cherednik operators under the two natural rescalings by positive and negative powers of the parameter $t$. We prove that these rescaled Cherednik operators admit well-defined limits on the ring of almost symmetric Laurent polynomials. This yields stable positive and negative actions of a common stable limit DAHA. The action of the limit Cherednik operators is also proven to be triangular on a natural basis of almost symmetric Laurent polynomials labeled by tuple-partition symbols with respect to the induced Bruhat order. We further construct for each of the two stable limit representations a set of simultaneous eigenfunctions of the limit Cherednik operators using the partial symmetrization operators acting on the non-symmetric Koornwinder polynomials. We show that each of the two sets of the eigenfunctions form a basis of the space of almost symmetric Laurent polynomials, and denote them by the positive and negative stable limit Koornwinder polynomials.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为广义Reedy范畴建立统一的表示论框架，将复杂的同调构造简化为初等线性代数和谱图论问题，并统一代数拓扑和表示论中看似分散的经典结果。

Method: 采用表示论方法，通过标准模之间的同态空间系统研究，利用关联矩阵和态射纤维将抽象同调构造约化为线性代数和谱图论。

Result: 建立了根树范畴（包括有限链、有限集与部分单射、有限蜘蛛等）上Dold-Kan对应的统一扩展，统一了Kuhn分解定理和Thévenaz-Webb半单性定理。

Abstract: We develop a representation-theoretic approach to generalized Reedy categories through a systematic study of homomorphism spaces between standard modules. For a broad class of these categories, we provide a uniform, computable framework that reduces abstract homological constructions to elementary linear algebra and spectral graph theory via incidence matrices and morphism fibers. As a primary application, we establish a uniform extension of the Dold--Kan correspondence for categories arising from rooted trees, encompassing the categories of finite chains, finite sets and partial injections, and finite spiders. Crucially, this machinery unifies and provides a singular conceptual basis for several classic, seemingly disparate results across algebraic topology and representation theory, including Kuhn's decomposition theorem for vector spaces and the Thévenaz--Webb semisimplicity theorem for Mackey functors.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we compute the Möbius function on the set of pseudo-Levi subgroups containing a fixed maximal torus of two families of finite groups, $GL_n(q)$ and $Sp_{2n}(q)$, for some natural number $n$ and a prime power $q$. The Möbius function for the set of pseudo-Levi subgroups of a finite group $G$ is important for an explicit evaluation of the formula expressing the decomposition of tensor products of characters, as proved in Theorem 2 of the paper ''Multiplicity of characters of finite reductive groups and Drinfeld doubles'' [arXiv:2512.01432].

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在证明归一化Witten zeta函数的极点性质及留数公式，并证实Au的伽马乘积形式猜想。

Method: 通过将主导格系数与收敛的球面Coxeter判别式积分关联，利用Macdonald-Mehta-Opdam恒等式的边界极点计算该积分，并证明真抛物型地层是严格次临界的。

Result: 证明了Au的归一化Witten zeta函数在s=2/h处有简单极点，留数为\frac{2(2π)^{r/2}\sqrt{\det C_Φ}}{h|W|}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r-1}Γ(1-d_i/h)}{Γ(1-1/h)^r}；获得了每个简单类型下维数不超过X的不可约表示数量的显式渐近常数。

Conclusion: 证实了Au的伽马乘积形式猜想及其在类型A_4中的预测，并给出了非Tauberian的直接渐近结果。

Abstract: Let $Φ$ be an irreducible crystallographic root system of rank $r$, with Coxeter number $h$, Weyl group $W$, Cartan matrix $C_Φ$, and invariant degrees $2=d_1\leq\cdots\leq d_r=h$. We prove that Au's normalized Witten zeta function $ξ_Φ(s)$ has a simple pole at $s=2/h$, with residue $\mathop{\rm Res}_{s=2/h}ξ_Φ(s)=\frac{2(2π)^{r/2}\sqrt{\det C_Φ}}{h|W|}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r-1}Γ(1-d_i/h)}{Γ(1-1/h)^r}$. The proof identifies the leading lattice coefficient with a convergent spherical Coxeter-discriminant integral at the critical exponent and evaluates this integral using the boundary pole of the Macdonald--Mehta--Opdam identity. Proper parabolic strata are shown to be strictly subcritical. This establishes Au's gamma-product-shape conjecture and his prediction in type $A_4$. We also obtain a direct, non-Tauberian asymptotic, with an explicit constant for every simple type, for the number of irreducible representations of dimension at most $X$.

</details>

### [59] [EFLUX: Elastic Multi-Robot Formation Navigation and Adaptation with Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12050)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Guangyao Shi,Jonathan Diller,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EFLUX是一个基于几何信息的大语言模型智能体框架，通过联合推理变形（缩放、剪切）和重构（分裂、合并）行为，实现多机器人编队在受限环境中的弹性导航，实验表明其能减少死锁和导航失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将编队变形和重构行为独立建模或通过手工规则连接，缺乏明确的几何判据来决定何时调用何种行为，导致在复杂环境中易产生次优轨迹和死锁问题。

Method: EFLUX框架包含三个核心模块：1) 结构化场景表示提取；2) 利用LLM联合推理变形行为（缩放、剪切）和重构行为（分裂、合并）；3) 通过闭环生成-验证-校正流水线将策略转化为可执行的单机器人航点。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验表明，EFLUX在受限环境下实现了安全、连续且弹性的编队导航，相比基线方法减少了死锁和导航失败，同时保持了连贯的多机器人协调。

Conclusion: 基于几何信息的LLM智能体框架能够有效统一编队变形与重构两种互补行为，实现复杂的多机器人编队导航任务。

Abstract: Multi-robot teams operating in confined or cluttered environments must adapt both their formation geometry and group topology to navigate through complex obstacles. This adaptation requires two complementary behaviors: deformation, where the team continuously reshapes its geometry while remaining connected, and reconfiguration, where robots split into subgroups or merge back into a single formation. Existing methods often model these behaviors independently, connect them through handcrafted rules, or lack explicit geometric criteria for determining when each behavior should be invoked. However, challenging environments may require online changes in formation shape, connectivity, and effective team composition, making decoupled or rule-based approaches prone to suboptimal trajectories and deadlock. We propose EFLUX, a geometry-grounded LLM agentic framework for automatic and elastic multi-robot formation navigation. EFLUX extracts a structured scene representation and uses an LLM to reason jointly over both deformation actions, such as scaling and shearing, and reconfiguration actions, such as splitting and merging. These strategies are then translated into executable per-robot waypoints through a closed-loop generation, verification, and correction pipeline. Simulation and hardware experiments show that EFLUX enables safe, continuous, and elastic formation navigation in constrained environments, reducing deadlock and navigation failures compared with baselines while maintaining coherent multi-robot coordination.

</details>


### [60] [Enabling 24-hour Agricultural Robotics: Unsupervised Day-to-Night Cross-Modal Image Translation for Nighttime Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12065)
*Robel Mamo,Rajitha de Silva,Grzegorz Cielniak,Taeyeong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督图像翻译框架，利用CLIP模型将白天农作物行RGB图像转换为近红外夜间图像，无需像素级标注即可训练夜间感知模型，并在真实机器人上验证了夜间视觉导航的有效性，同时发布了首个夜间农业视觉导航基准数据集AgriNight。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业视觉导航系统大多假设白天条件，而夜间部署机器人能实现24小时监测和作业，但基于视觉的夜间系统缺乏大规模标注数据集，难以直接训练感知模型。

Method: 提出无监督图像翻译框架，将白天RGB图像转换为近红外夜间图像；引入预训练CLIP模型保持语义一致性；设计可见性掩码处理近红外照明有限有效范围的问题；无需像素级配对监督即可复用白天语义标签。

Result: 与多种先进图像翻译基线相比，该方法生成的图像质量更高；在夜间语义分割下游任务中表现更优；在真实机器人上实现了实时自主夜间导航。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了夜间农业视觉导航中缺乏标注数据的问题，AgriNight数据集为后续研究提供了基准。

Abstract: While visual navigation has been extensively studied in agricultural robotics, most existing systems assume daytime conditions. In fact, deploying autonomous robots at night offers significant advantages, including 24-hour crop and soil monitoring, fruit harvesting, and nocturnal pest detection. Modern vision-based systems, however, rely heavily on large-scale well-annotated image datasets, which remains challenging to obtain for nighttime operation scenarios. To address this, we propose an unsupervised image translation framework that converts daytime plant-row RGB images into near-infrared (NIR) nighttime counterparts without requiring pixel-to-pixel supervision. This enables the direct reuse of daytime semantic labels for training nighttime perception models. In particular, by incorporating a pre-trained Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) model, the proposed framework is designed to preserve semantic consistency during day-to-night translation. Additionally, a visibility mask is introduced to account for the limited effective range of NIR illumination in nighttime scenes. We conduct comparative evaluations with state-of-the-art image translation baselines and demonstrate higher image qualities, as supported by improved performance in downstream semantic segmentation for nighttime visual navigation. For evaluation, we utilize AgriNight--a novel dataset comprising 428 daytime and 549 nighttime images collected using night-vision-equipped mobile robots in agricultural fields and manually annotated with pixel-wise semantic labels--and introduce it as the first benchmark for nighttime agricultural visual navigation. We also perform real-time autonomous navigation experiments with a physical robot operating at night. The data and code are available at: https://github.com/mamorobel/AgriNight.

</details>


### [61] [Robust In-Hand Manipulation via Priors in Reinforcement Learning and Mechanical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12105)
*Yifei Chen,Shihan Lu,Ed Colgate,Kevin Lynch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补的物理先验（全局抓取质量先验和局部接触几何先验），用于强化学习训练多指灵巧手进行稳健的掌上滚动操作，显著提升了旋转效率、抓取稳定性和抗干扰能力，并改善了sim-to-real迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无外部传感的掌上操作因指-物接触不确定性和重力干扰而极具挑战性，现有强化学习方法未优先考虑维持良好条件的抓取以支持持续操作。

Method: 引入两种互补物理先验：(1) 全局抓取质量先验——基于经典抓取分析，作为密集奖励塑形项，鼓励良好分布的接触并改善最坏情况下的力旋量抵抗；(2) 局部接触几何先验——基于指尖曲率，通过指尖几何形状机械地塑造接触界面，使其朝向任务对齐的滚动并减少离轴漂移。

Result: 在多指灵巧手上对三种物体、四种手掌朝向的掌上滚动操作进行评估，结果显示在旋转效率、抓取稳定性和抗干扰能力方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 嵌入学习和指尖形态中的物理先验能够提高任务鲁棒性和sim-to-real迁移效果。

Abstract: In-hand manipulation without external sensing is challenging due to uncertainties from finger-object contacts and disturbances by gravity. While reinforcement learning has shown promise in learning complex finger gaiting, existing approaches do not prioritize maintaining well-conditioned grasps for sustained manipulation. We introduce two complementary physics priors for robust in-hand rolling: a global grasp-quality prior derived from classical grasp analysis and a local contact-geometry prior based on fingertip curvature. The grasp-quality prior is used as a dense reward-shaping term that encourages well-distributed contacts with improved worst-case wrench resistance. The contact-geometry prior is expressed in the fingertip geometry that mechanically shapes the contact interface toward task-aligned rolling while reducing off-axis drift. We evaluate the effect of these priors on learning in-hand rolling manipulation for a multifingered robotic hand manipulating three different objects at four palm orientations. Results show significant improvement in rotation efficiency, grasp stability, and disturbance rejection, suggesting that physics priors embedded in both learning and fingertip morphology improve task robustness and sim-to-real transfer. An overview video can be found at https://youtu.be/pdd1wHxQnJM?si=dM-U5kiiPTYsk3Pk.

</details>


### [62] [GaitSpan: Growing Humanoid Locomotion from Walking to Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12114)
*Kwan-Yee Lin,Zilin Wang,Janelle J. Liu,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaitSpan通过将预训练的行走策略扩展为更快的 locomotion，实现了单一策略在连续速度范围内覆盖行走、慢跑和跑步，无需重新学习，并支持跨形态和零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在获取步态多样性时通常需要预设步态计划、模仿运动片段或训练多个专家策略，缺乏对连续速度指令、不同地形和形态的灵活适应性。作者希望实现从基础行走策略自然扩展到更快 locomotion 的能力，避免从头学习。

Method: 提出GaitSpan框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 节奏生成（rhythm generation）——通过多个内部时钟调制冻结的行走策略，学习指令条件化的规范动作组合；2) 步幅塑形（stride shaping）——基于弹簧负载倒立摆（SLIP）动力学的物理目标奖励动态 locomotion 模式；3) 残差适应（residual adaptation）——捕获前两者未覆盖的运动细节。

Result: GaitSpan是首个实现单一指令条件化人形策略覆盖行走、慢跑和跑步连续速度范围的方法；支持跨形态迁移；在未见过的 sim-to-sim 和真实地形上实现零样本部署；相比多专家训练或人类模仿基线，学习更快且步态性能更强。

Conclusion: 将行走作为种子技能（seed skill），通过节奏调制、物理目标引导和残差补偿三个维度的扩展，可以有效地从基础步态生长出更复杂的 locomotion 能力，为连续速度范围的步态生成提供了灵活的框架。

Abstract: A humanoid that can walk should not relearn locomotion from scratch to jog or run. Yet current approaches often obtain gait diversity by prescribing gait schedules, imitating motion clips, training experts to switch between or distilling skills into one policy. These strategies can produce impressive behaviors, but offer limited flexibility across continuous speed commands, terrains, and morphologies. We study skill growth with GaitSpan, a framework that expands a pretrained, basic walking policy into faster locomotion. It treats walking as a seed skill: reusable motor structure for balance, support, body coordination, and contact transition that can be regenerated at new rhythms, extended into longer/higher strides, and corrected by residual adaptation. This expansion has three aspects: 1) rhythm generation, which modulates the frozen walking policy with multiple internal clocks and learns command-conditioned combinations of the resulting canonical actions; 2) stride shaping, which rewards dynamic locomotion patterns appropriate for higher commanded speeds using a physically grounded objective inspired by spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics; and 3) residual adaptation, which captures motion details not accounted for by rhythm generation or stride shaping. GaitSpan is the first to deliver a single command-conditioned humanoid policy that spans walking, jogging, and running-like regimes covering a continuous speed range, transfers across morphologies, and deploys zero-shot on unseen sim-to-sim, and real-world terrains. Compared with baselines either trained with multi-experts or imitation from humans, it learns faster and achieves stronger gait performance.

</details>


### [63] [A Behavioral State Vocabulary in Sony ERS-111 R-CODE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12115)
*Christopher A. Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对索尼ERS-111 AIBO的R-CODE样本库中的行为图进行语料级分析，发现表面多样的例程实际上由一套以初始化、感知、迭代动作、同步和恢复为核心的紧凑具身语法构建，并论证这种基于状态的抽象可作为构建新行为例程的中间表示，特别适用于资源受限的机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对AIBO行为脚本的研究多为孤立的脚本分析，缺乏从语料库层面系统性地审视样本集中行为的公共结构，导致对行为构建底层语法的理解不足。

Method: 对索尼R-CODE样本分布中生成的行为图进行语料级分析，跨脚本比较命名状态，识别构建样本集的重复控制词汇，并在聚合层面揭示其底层语法结构。

Result: 发现表面不同的例程主要由初始化、感知、迭代动作、同步和恢复等有限的具身语法构建而成，形成紧凑的控制词汇集合。

Conclusion: 这种基于状态的抽象形式可作为构建新封装行为例程的中间表示，特别适用于需要确定性控制、直接硬件访问和模块化行为组合的受限原生机器人系统。

Abstract: This paper presents a corpus-level analysis of generated behavior diagrams derived from Sony's R-CODE sample distribution for the ERS-111 AIBO. Rather than reading each script in isolation, the study compares named states across the corpus to identify the recurring control vocabulary that structures the sample set. The resulting aggregate shows that many superficially different routines are built from a compact embodied grammar centered on initialization, sensing, iterative action, synchronization, and recovery. In addition to historical analysis, the paper argues that this form of state-based abstraction is useful as an intermediate representation for constructing new encapsulated behavior routines, especially on constrained native robotic systems where deterministic control, direct hardware access, and modular behavioral composition remain important.

</details>


### [64] [More than a Manipulator: Planning Propellant-Free Attitude Maneuvers for Free-Floating Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12130)
*Harsh G. Bhundiya,Avi Soval,Keenan Albee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用空间机械臂运动实现航天器无工质姿态控制的方法，通过轨迹优化和避碰约束求解非线性规划问题，验证了机械臂作为冗余或主姿态控制系统的潜力，特别适用于在轨组装与制造场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统航天器姿态控制依赖动量交换装置或消耗推进剂的推力器，而机械臂通常被视为扰动源而非执行机构。本文旨在探索利用机械臂运动实现无工质姿态控制的可能性。

Method: 将姿态控制问题建模为包含关节约束和碰撞避免约束的轨迹优化问题，采用内点法求解非线性规划，对比了不同运动学复杂度与质量特性的航天器-机械臂系统，并通过动量/力矩包络与反作用飞轮阵列进行直接比较。

Result: 成功演示了复杂消旋和姿态机动轨迹；机械臂在控制能力上可与反作用飞轮阵列相当，具备作为冗余甚至主姿态控制系统的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作为将机械臂用作多用途姿态控制执行机构提供了框架，在抓取高相对质量比载荷的在轨组装与制造任务中尤其具有应用前景。

Abstract: Spacecraft attitude control is traditionally achieved using momentum exchange devices or propellant-consuming thrusters. Meanwhile, a growing number of missions require robotic manipulators, which are typically treated as disturbance sources to be rejected rather than as actuators for spacecraft reorientation. This work investigates the use of manipulator motions for propellant-free attitude control by formulating a trajectory optimization problem with critical joint and collision avoidance constraints. Using an interior point solver for the resulting nonlinear program, complex slew and detumble trajectories are demonstrated for a range of spacecraft-manipulator systems with varying kinematic complexity and mass properties. The achievable control authority is compared directly with that of reaction wheel arrays via momentum and torque envelopes, demonstrating the potential for manipulators to serve as redundant or even primary attitude control systems. This work provides a framework for using manipulators as multipurpose attitude control actuators, with particularly promising applications in in-space assembly and manufacturing when grasping payloads with high relative mass fractions.

</details>


### [65] [Analysis of Mutual and Referential Human and Robot Gazes in a Collaborative Word Association Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12181)
*Jens V. Rüppel,Tim Schreiter,Andrey Rudenko,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人凝视在人机协作游戏中对人类视觉注意的影响，发现机器人凝视方向不影响参与者对单词的首次注视时间，但参与者确认请求时更频繁地凝视机器人，表明任务的语言方面压倒了机器人参照凝视的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数关于人机对话中凝视的研究聚焦于面对面社交对话，而对任务导向型交互中的凝视行为知之甚少。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究机器人凝视在需要协调和高效沟通的协作任务中如何影响人类视觉注意。

Method: 让参与者与NAO机器人（作为具身化、LLM驱动的对话伙伴）玩协作性词语联想游戏，在两种条件下（机器人分别实施相互凝视和参照凝视）进行实验，使用眼动追踪眼镜记录凝视数据，并通过凝视坐标、语音片段、关键事件和兴趣区域进行分析。

Result: 机器人凝视方向不影响参与者首次注视机器人提议单词的时间；参与者在包含确认请求的对话行中更频繁地凝视机器人；由于游戏的高认知需求，语言方面压倒了机器人参照凝视的效果。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计协作任务中机器人凝视和轮流发言行为提供了有价值的见解，有助于需要协调和高效沟通的场景中机器人行为的设计与验证。

Abstract: Robot gaze is a major component of human-robot dialogue coordination. Most studies of gaze in human-robot dialogue focus on face-to-face social conversations, but little is known about gaze in demanding task-focused interactions. In this paper, we investigate how the gaze of a robot game partner affects human visual attention and if humans tend to direct confirmation-seeking gazes towards the robot. In our study, we let participants play a collaborative word association game with a NAO robot acting as an embodied, LLM-driven conversational partner. Our experiments are conducted under two conditions, which implement mutual and referential gazes of the robot respectively. We record participants' gaze using eye tracking glasses and analyze the interactions using gaze coordinates, speech segments, key events and areas of interests. We find that robot gaze orientation does not affect the time to first fixation on words the robot proposed. We also find that participants gaze more often at the robot when their dialogue line contains confirmation requests, compared to when it does not. Our results indicate (likely also due to the cognitively demanding nature of the game) that the verbal aspect of this task overshadows the effects of referential robot gaze. These findings offer valuable insights for designing and validating robot gaze and turn-taking behavior in collaborative tasks which require coordination and efficient communication.

</details>


### [66] [Contract-Grounded Behavior Tree Synthesis via Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12220)
*Jonathan Salfity,Robert Blake Anderson,Mitch Pryor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于契约约束的BT合成架构，通过MCP服务器让编码代理获取机器人技能库、BT操作符和组合模板，实现从自然语言到可执行行为树的可靠生成，在110个仿真任务和14个实体机器人任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的BT合成方法将接地（grounding）责任推给提示词编写者，导致当编写者不了解机器人实际可执行技能、技能参数化方式或运行时软件对BT结构的约束时，部署变得脆弱不可靠。

Method: 提出契约接地BT合成架构：编码代理在生成BT前，通过查询机器人端MCP服务器获取显式契约（包括技能库、允许的BT操作符、可选BT组合模板），再合成BT；随后由机器人运行时验证门（validation gate）在执行前强制校验正确性。

Result: 契约接地实现接近完美的BT验证和高任务成功率；BT组合模板显著提升了较小模型在反应式控制流任务上的表现；架构可迁移到运行Nav2栈的物理硬件上，且Nav2对操作员和代理均不透明。

Conclusion: 契约接地机制能有效解决LLM在机器人行为树生成中的接地问题，降低对操作员专业知识的要求，提高部署鲁棒性，并适用于不同规模的LLM及实体机器人平台。

Abstract: Synthesizing deployable robot behavior trees (BTs) from natural language (NL) requires grounding to ensure every generated BT references only skills a robot can actually execute. Existing LLM-based BT synthesis approaches often place this grounding responsibility on the prompt author. This makes deployment brittle when the author does not know which skills the robot can execute, how those skills are parameterized, or how the robot runtime software constrains valid BT structure. This paper proposes a contract-grounded BT synthesis architecture in which a coding agent queries a robot-side Model Context Protocol (MCP) server to retrieve an explicit contract consisting of a skill library, permitted BT operators, and optional BT composition templates, before synthesizing a BT for validation and execution. In our framework, non-expert operators issue NL commands without knowledge of robot implementation details, while a robot runtime validation gate enforces correctness before execution. We evaluate two LLMs, a closed model (Sonnet 4.6) and a smaller open-source model (Gemma4:31b), across 110 simulated tasks in PyRoboSim and 14 tasks on a physical Husarion Panther robot. Results show that contract grounding enables near-perfect BT validation and high task success, that BT composition templates substantially recover success on reactive control-flow tasks for the smaller model, and that the architecture transfers to physical hardware running a Nav2 stack opaque to both operator and agent.

</details>


### [67] [DiffRadar: Differentiable Physics-Aware Radar SLAM with Gaussian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12265)
*Gaurav Bagwe,Xiaoyong Yuan,Yongji Wu,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffRadar 是一种实时雷达SLAM系统，将雷达观测建模为可微分的、物理感知的高斯场而非离散扫描，通过各向异性高斯原语和可微分雷达前向模型联合优化机器人位姿与场景结构，在公开基准和压力测试中显著降低轨迹误差、提升地图一致性，并保持70 FPS的实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达SLAM系统通过在离散化雷达热图上进行扫描匹配来估计运动，破坏了几何连续性且未能捕捉关键的雷达感知特性，导致在退化或动态变化环境中位姿估计不稳定、建图质量下降。

Method: 将场景表示为各向异性高斯原语（anisotropic Gaussian primitives），通过可微分雷达前向模型在距离-方位角和多普勒-方位角空间中渲染雷达测量值，实现从雷达测量中联合优化机器人位姿和场景结构。

Result: 在Radarize公开基准上显著降低轨迹误差，在特征匮乏的走廊运动场景中提升尤为突出；地图一致性提升超过一倍；在商用FMCW雷达硬件上保持70 FPS的实时性能。

Conclusion: 在信号域直接对雷达观测进行建模，能够为移动平台实现显著更鲁棒和一致的纯雷达SLAM。

Abstract: Radar sensing is increasingly used in mobile systems because it operates reliably under poor lighting, adverse weather, and privacy-sensitive settings where cameras and LiDAR often fail. However, most existing radar SLAM systems estimate motion through scan matching on discretized radar heatmaps, which breaks geometric continuity and fails to capture key radar sensing properties, often leading to unstable pose estimation and degraded mapping in regenerate or dynamically changing environments. We present DiffRadar, a real-time radar SLAM system that models radar observations as a differentiable, physics-aware Gaussian field rather than discrete scans. DiffRadar represents the scene as anisotropic Gaussian primitives and renders radar measurements in range-azimuth and Doppler-azimuth spaces through a differentiable radar forward model, enabling joint optimization of robot pose and scene structure directly from radar measurements. We implement DiffRadar on commodity FMCW radar hardware and evaluate it on both the public Radarize benchmark and a controlled stress-test suite that targets common radar SLAM failure modes, including corridor degeneracy, motion regime transitions, dynamic clutter, and long-horizon loop closures. DiffRadar achieves substantial reductions in trajectory error on the benchmark, with especially large gains under feature-poor corridor motion, while more than doubling map consistency and maintaining real-time performance at 70 FPS. These results show that modeling radar observations directly in the signal domain enables substantially more robust and consistent radar-only SLAM for mobile platforms.

</details>


### [68] [Flatness-Preserving Residual Learning for Real-Time Tight Quadrotor Formation Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12275)
*Pei-An Hsieh,Fengjun Yang,Nikolai Matni,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息残差动力学学习框架，用于补偿四旋翼紧密编队飞行中的气动干扰（如下洗气流），同时保持系统微分平坦性，从而支持高效的反馈线性化控制。硬件实验表明，该方法将跟踪误差平均降低31%，计算量比NMPC低一个数量级，仅需30秒训练数据和5ms循环周期即可实现稳定编队飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在紧密编队飞行时会受到严重的气动湍流干扰（如下洗气流），若未建模可能导致灾难性碰撞。现有方法难以在计算受限的平台上实现高保真气动补偿。

Method: 提出物理信息残差动力学学习框架，在捕捉复杂气动相互作用的同时确保多四旋翼联合系统保持微分平坦性；利用保留的平坦性设计反馈线性化控制器，通过前馈补偿消除气动扰动。

Result: 硬件实验显示平均跟踪误差降低31%；跟踪性能与NMPC相当，但计算量降低一个数量级；仅需30秒训练数据和5ms循环周期即可实现稳定紧密编队飞行。

Conclusion: 该轻量级方法首次证明可在极低训练数据量和计算资源下实现高保真气动补偿的稳定紧密编队飞行，适用于计算受限的飞行平台。

Abstract: Quadrotors flying in tight formations are severely affected by turbulent aerodynamic interactions, such as downwash, that can cause catastrophic collisions if left unmodeled. To compensate for these effects, we propose a physics-informed residual dynamics learning framework that captures complex aerodynamic interactions while ensuring the joint multi-quadrotor system remains differentially flat. We leverage this preserved flatness to design a computationally efficient feedback linearization controller that is easily tunable with linear control techniques and cancels aerodynamic disturbances via feedforward compensation. Hardware experiments demonstrate our framework reduces average tracking errors by 31% compared to nominal baselines. Crucially, our lightweight approach matches the tracking performance of state-of-the-art nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) while requiring an order of magnitude less computation. We are the first to show that stable, tight formation flight can be achieved with under 30 seconds of training data and a 5ms loop rate, unlocking high-fidelity aerodynamic compensation for compute-constrained flight stacks.

</details>


### [69] [Reducing Temporal Redundancy for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12287)
*Yuzhou Wu,Yuxin Zheng,Muchun Niu,Yishan Yang,Tianhao Liu,hanwen kang,Jiajian Jing,Linfeng Zhang,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA模型系统级加速策略，通过增量感知更新和2步扩散采样压缩，实现2倍以上加速且保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型推理延迟高，无法满足实时部署需求，主要源于连续帧的重复视觉编码和扩散策略的多步迭代采样两大时间冗余来源。

Method: 感知端：仅增量更新动态场景区域的token，而非重新编码整帧；策略端：通过效率导向训练将扩散采样压缩为紧凑的2步调度，同时保持动作精度。

Result: 在Libero、RobotWin和真实机器人平台上实现2倍以上加速，在通用操作基准上高达98%的成功率。

Conclusion: 系统级加速策略有效降低VLA模型推理延迟，兼顾实时性与性能，代码将开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit strong generalization for robotic manipulation, yet their high inference latency limits real time deployment. We identify two primary sources of temporal redundancy in existing VLA pipelines: repeated visual encoding of highly similar consecutive frames and multi step iterative sampling in diffusion based policies. To address this, we propose a system level acceleration strategy that reduces computation in both perception and action generation. On the perception side, we incrementally update only tokens corresponding to dynamic scene regions instead of re-encoding entire frames. On the policy side, we compress diffusion sampling into a compact 2-step schedule through efficiency oriented training while preserving action precision. Experiments on Libero, RobotWin, and Real Robot Platforms demonstrate over 2 times speedup while maintaining high performance, achieving up to 98% success rate on general manipulation benchmarks. Our codes will be released on Github.

</details>


### [70] [VistaVLA: Geometry- and Semantic-Aware 3D Gaussian-Grounded VLA for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12356)
*Mohan Liu,Zhihao Gu,Xuanyu Chen,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Yan Wu,Wei-Yun Yau,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VistaVLA提出两阶段框架，通过3D高斯原语构建几何-语义联合的3D认知表征，并利用Merge-then-Query (MtQ)机制压缩为紧凑上下文令牌用于VLA策略学习，在真实世界任务中提升22.8%成功率，在分布外任务上超越VLA-Adapter基线30.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏显式的场景级3D表示，限制了空间布局和几何约束的推理能力；而引入深度图或点云的方案仅捕获低层结构，缺乏高层3D语义。受人类认知中3D语义认知地图启发，旨在构建兼具几何与语义的3D认知表征以提升VLA策略学习。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 将多视图视觉-语言特征提升为3D高斯原语，形成几何锚定的语义令牌，对齐视角一致的3D空间与2D视觉特征空间；(2) 提出MtQ (Merge-then-Query)令牌摘要机制，将密集高斯原语压缩为高度紧凑的空间信息令牌，实现99%令牌缩减同时保留动作相关的3D布局与语义。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境均验证有效性。真实场景中，7项任务成功率提升22.8%；在挑战性分布外任务中，相对VLA-Adapter基线提升30.0%。

Conclusion: VistaVLA通过构建几何-语义感知的3D认知表征并高效压缩，显著增强了VLA模型的空间推理与泛化能力，为机器人操作提供了更接近人类认知的3D语义理解框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful end-to-end paradigm for robotic manipulation by mapping language instructions and 2D visual inputs directly to actions. However, these models lack an explicit, scene-level 3D representation, limiting their ability to reason over spatial layouts and geometric constraints. While recent efforts incorporate explicit 3D cues, such as depth maps or point clouds, to improve geometric awareness, they primarily capture low-level structures and lack high-level semantic grounding in 3D space. In human cognition, interaction with the physical world relies on a 3D semantic cognitive map - an internal mental model that integrates spatial layouts with semantic context to enable persistent, viewpoint-invariant reasoning. In light of this, we present VistaVLA, a novel two-stage framework that constructs a geometry- and semantics-aware 3D cognitive representation from 3D Gaussian primitives and grounds it as compact context tokens for VLA policy learning. Specifically, VistaVLA lifts multi-view vision-language features into 3D Gaussian primitives, forming geometry-anchored semantic tokens that align view-consistent spatial grounding with 2D visual feature spaces. To make this 3D representation computationally tractable for effective VLA control, we introduce Merge-then-Query (MtQ), a token summarization mechanism. MtQ compresses dense Gaussian primitives into a highly compact set of spatially informative tokens, achieving a 99% token reduction while preserving action-relevant 3D layouts and semantic context. Extensive evaluations in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness of VistaVLA. Notably, in real-world scenarios, VistaVLA improves success rates by 22.8% across seven real-world tasks and by 30.0% over the VLA-Adapter baseline on challenging out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [71] [StratMamba: Strategic and Reactive Stream Partitioning for Path-Efficient LiDAR-Based Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12370)
*Hung-Chieh Wu,Xiaopan Zhang,Kasra Sinaei,Ryan Abnavi,Kasun Weerakoon,Christopher Bradley,Seyed Fakoorian,Jiachen Li,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出StratMamba，一种双流Mamba时序建模架构，结合快衰减与慢衰减记忆，分别处理高频LiDAR数据用于反应式避障和长程目标信息用于策略规划，在仿真和真实的Unitree GO1四足机器人上实现了更优的时序推理效率、最快导航速度（576中位数步数，比Vanilla-Mamba提升5.0%）和最高路径最优性（0.915路径效率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如LSTM、Transformer、Vanilla-Mamba）在复杂障碍环境中难以高效捕获机器人导航所需的长时间依赖关系，需要一种既能快速反应避障又能保持长程策略规划的时序建模架构。

Method: 提出StratMamba双流Mamba架构：快衰减流处理高频LiDAR数据用于反应式避障，慢衰减流维持长程目标信息用于策略规划；在IsaacLab和Gazebo中仿真评估，并在Unitree GO1四足机器人上完成sim-to-real部署。

Result: 相比LSTM、Transformer、Vanilla-Mamba等基线，StratMamba实现了最低超时率、最快导航速度（576中位数步骤，比Vanilla-Mamba提升5.0%）、最高路径最优性（0.915路径效率）；真实场景中在扩展LiDAR范围内性能更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 双流记忆分区策略能有效平衡反应式安全与策略性导航，在具有挑战性的感知条件下显著优于单流Mamba和Transformer基线。

Abstract: This paper proposes StratMamba, a dual-stream Mamba-based temporal modeling architecture, to more efficiently capture long-horizon temporal dependencies required for robot navigation in complex and obstacle-rich environments. StratMamba leverages a combination of fast-decay and slow-decay memory architectures, where the fast-decay component processes high-frequency LiDAR data for reactive obstacle avoidance, while the slow-decay component maintains longer-horizon goal information for strategic planning. We perform extensive evaluations of different obstacle avoidance scenarios in IsaacLab and Gazebo, while also validating successful sim-to-real deployment on a Unitree GO1 quadruped robot navigating in the presence of static/dynamic obstacles. Comparisons with other temporal RL baselines, such as LSTM, Transformer, and Vanilla-Mamba, show that our StratMamba achieves exceptional temporal reasoning efficiency with a lower timeout rate, while maintaining the fastest navigation speed (576 median steps, 5.0% better than Vanilla-Mamba). It also achieves the highest path optimality (0.915 path efficiency) across all baselines. Real-world evaluation reveals that StratMamba maintains more robust performance across extended LiDAR ranges compared to vanilla Mamba and the Transformer, demonstrating that dual-stream partitioning effectively balances reactive safety with strategic navigation under challenging sensing conditions.

</details>


### [72] [Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control for Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12423)
*Zhilin He,Yorai Shaoul,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MDOC，一种基于模型的扩散规划器，无需数据即可生成动态可行、无碰撞的多机器人轨迹，通过结合已知动力学模型与控制障碍函数约束投影，并利用冲突搜索实现多机器人场景扩展，在样本效率、几何平滑度和成功率上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的轨迹规划方法依赖大量演示数据，且难以在采样过程中严格施加动力学可行性和硬安全约束，需要一种无需数据、能严格保证约束的多机器人运动规划方法。

Method: 提出MDOC，使用基于模型的扩散规划器，结合已知动力学模型和CBF（控制障碍函数）约束投影确保安全，通过Conflict-Based Search将安全机制自然扩展到多机器人规划场景。

Result: 在仿真实验中，MDOC在样本效率、几何平滑度、成功率上一致优于基线规划器，同时减少了计算时间并生成了无碰撞轨迹。

Conclusion: MDOC有效解决了数据依赖和约束难以严格满足的问题，为多机器人运动规划提供了一种高效、安全且无需演示数据的扩散规划方法。

Abstract: Multi-Robot Motion Planning in continuous environments, where robots must generate dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories, is challenging due to the combinatorial growth of the joint trajectory space and the difficulty of enforcing dynamic feasibility and hard safety constraints. Recent approaches recast trajectory planning as probabilistic inference, sampling from a posterior over trajectories using diffusion models whose score functions are learned from demonstration data. While showing promising performance, these approaches are limited: they often rely on sizable demonstration datasets and struggle to rigorously enforce dynamics and hard safety constraints during sampling. To this end, we introduce Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control (MDOC), a model-based diffusion planner that efficiently produces dynamically feasible trajectories without relying on data. Crucially, we show that MDOC's safety mechanism -- combining known dynamics models with Control Barrier Function-constrained projections -- naturally scales to multi-robot planning settings through Conflict-Based Search. Across simulation experiments, this integrated method consistently outperforms representative baseline planners in sample efficiency, geometric smoothness, and success rate, while reducing computation time and producing collision-free trajectories.

</details>


### [73] [Deployable Human Preference Alignment in Robotics: Learning Representative Rewards from Diverse Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12466)
*Taehyung Kim,Gwangmo Lee,Minjun Chang,Sunghyun Lim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PREC框架通过聚类用户偏好，从稀疏、带噪声的二元偏好标签中学习一组紧凑的策略，在减少部署验证负担的同时提升社会福利指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，每用户对齐策略面临反馈稀疏、易受噪声影响且验证困难的问题；而单一共享策略无法捕捉异构偏好且忽视少数群体偏好。

Method: PREC先忽略偏好标签，聚合所有用户轨迹学习共享轨迹编码器；然后利用该表示联合进行用户聚类（偏好一致簇）并学习每个簇的代表性奖励模型，最终为每个簇优化策略。

Result: 在多种模拟 locomotion 环境中，PREC比基线更准确地将用户聚类到偏好一致的簇中；在稀疏和带噪声反馈下，PREC在所有三项社会福利指标上均优于现有单共享策略对齐方法，甚至超越每用户对齐方法。

Conclusion: PREC通过聚类相似用户补偿单用户标签不足并减轻标签噪声影响，同时保持可控数量的奖励模型以降低部署验证负担，实现了更优的用户偏好对齐。

Abstract: Aligning robot policies with human preferences is essential for deployment to diverse end users. In per-user alignment approach, preference feedback is often sparse, so learning becomes unstable and vulnerable to human preference noise, and a growing number of individualized policies makes validation difficult before deployment. A single shared policy approach to user alignment avoids this cost but fails to capture heterogeneous preferences and often neglects minority preferences. To address these challenges, we introduce Preference-based REward Clustering (PREC), a novel framework that learns a compact set of policies from binary preference labels provided by diverse users. From a dataset of user trajectories and their preference labels, PREC first sets the labels aside and aggregates trajectories across users to learn a population-level shared trajectory encoder, alleviating limited per-user coverage and avoiding label noise during representation learning. Using this representation, PREC jointly assigns users to preference-coherent clusters and learns a representative reward model per cluster using preference labels, from which a policy is optimized for each cluster. Clustering similar users compensates for the limited number of labels available from each user and mitigates the effect of label noise. At the same time, maintaining a manageable number of reward models reduces the validation burden at deployment. Experiments across diverse simulated locomotion environments show that PREC groups users who label different trajectory subsets into preference-coherent clusters more accurately than baseline methods. Under sparse and noisy feedback, policies trained with PREC improve all three social welfare metrics over an existing single shared-policy user-alignment approach and even outperform per-user alignment approaches.

</details>


### [74] [Infra-Swarm: Robust Vision-Based Multi-Robot Swarming via Near-Infrared Spectral Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12489)
*Haoyu Chen,Qijin Li,Wanyu Xiang,Xiuxiu Lin,Zian Ning,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Infra-Swarm，一种基于近红外光视觉的分布式集群系统，利用940nm窄带滤波和光斑3D定位实现厘米级邻居感知，兼顾硬件级鲁棒性和低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式集群依赖主动无线通信（带宽受限）或被动视觉（环境敏感），缺乏在资源受限硬件上兼具鲁棒性和可扩展性的感知方案。

Method: 每台机器人配备近红外光源和四颗灰度相机，结合940nm窄带滤波（抑制99.2%环境光干扰），通过图像中光斑的方位（bearing）和强度（strength）直接计算邻居的厘米级3D位置。

Result: 硬件级滤波使感知前端对环境光照变化具有强鲁棒性；计算开销极低，支持资源受限硬件上大规模集群的扩展。

Conclusion: Infra-Swarm为视觉集群提供一种鲁棒、轻量且可规模化的感知方案，平衡了环境适应性、计算效率与扩展性。

Abstract: Distributed swarms typically rely on either active wireless communication or passive vision, and they are frequently hindered by bandwidth constraints or environmental sensitivity. This paper proposes Infra-Swarm, a robust vision-based swarm. Each robot is equipped with a near-infrared light source and four ordinary gray-scale cameras. The Infra-Swarm system directly measures the centimeter-level 3D position of neighbors based on the position (bearing) and intensity (strength) of optical flares in the captured images. By utilizing 940 nm narrow-band filters to physically reject 99.2% of ambient light interference, the perception front-end achieves hardware-level robustness against illumination variations. Furthermore, its minimal computational overhead provides a resilient foundation for the massive scalability of robotic collectives on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [75] [A Bearing-Strength Method for Motion Estimation of Unknown Energy Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12515)
*Haoyu Chen,Zian Ning,Yin Zhang,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用被动传感器对移动能量发射器进行运动估计的新方法，通过联合使用方位角测量和接收信号强度信息，消除了传统仅依赖方位角方法所需的传感器横向运动条件，显著提升了系统可观性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仅依赖方位角（bearing-only）的运动估计方法存在一个根本性限制：传感器必须具有横向运动才能保证系统可观性，但这往往与许多任务中传感器的期望运动模式相冲突。论文旨在利用已被忽视的接收信号强度信息来克服这一限制。

Method: 提出一种结合方位角测量与接收信号强度的联合估计方法（bearing-strength method），充分利用两种易获取的测量信息进行运动估计，并通过理论分析证明该方法在可观性上的优势。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提方法显著增强了系统可观性，不再需要传感器具有横向运动。真实世界实验验证了该方法和理论分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘已被长期忽视的接收信号强度信息，无需任何额外成本即可大幅提升被动传感器对移动能量发射器的运动估计性能，突破传统方法的关键局限。

Abstract: This paper studies motion estimation of moving energy emitters using passive sensors. The emitters may be light, acoustic, or radio sources. While the bearing vector pointing from the sensor to the emitter can be easily obtained, existing approaches mainly rely on the bearing-only motion estimation method. However, this method suffers from a fundamental limitation that the sensor must have lateral motion to ensure observability. Unfortunately, this lateral motion requirement often conflicts with the sensor's desired motion in many tasks. In this paper, we point out that the received signal strength, which can also be obtained easily in many ways, can greatly enhance motion estimation. Surprisingly, this strength information has not been well explored so far. Here, we propose a new bearing-strength method to fully exploit both the bearing and strength measurements. Our theoretical analysis shows that the system observability is significantly enhanced in the sense that the lateral motion condition is not required anymore. Real-world experimental results verify the proposed method and the theoretical analysis. It is notable that the benefit of the proposed method comes with no additional cost since it simply utilizes the received strength information that has not been fully exploited in the past.

</details>


### [76] [Mind the Gap: Promises and Pitfalls of Hierarchical Planning in LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12547)
*Niccolò Caselli,Salvatore Lo Sardo,Francesco Massafra,Ippokratis Pantelidis,Samuele Punzo,Sathya Kamesh Bhethanabhotla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hi-LeWM通过在冻结的低层LeWM上添加高层规划来研究时间层次结构是否能改进长时域目标条件控制。结果显示，层次结构并非自动提升性能：短时域下单步高层规划最优，长时域下高层动作空间与推理搜索分布不匹配。通过约束搜索于训练轨迹编码的宏动作并适当调整子目标执行时序，Hi-LeWM在PushT中程和最长时域上分别比平坦LeWM提升11.3和14.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时间层次结构（temporal hierarchy）是否能改善LeWorldModel在长时域目标条件控制任务中的表现，即高层规划是否有助于提升长时域下的控制性能。

Method: 提出Hi-LeWM，冻结预训练的低层LeWM，在其上添加高层规划模块，通过潜在子目标进行高层规划。实验在PushT和Cube任务上评估不同目标偏移下的性能，并对比不同高层搜索策略（包括未约束搜索、约束搜索于训练轨迹宏动作等）。

Result: 短时域下单步高层规划最优；长时域下高层动作空间与推理搜索分布不匹配。使用真实未来潜在子目标时，冻结的低层控制器能执行良好对齐的中间目标，表明高层子目标生成是主要瓶颈。约束搜索于训练轨迹编码的宏动作并调整子目标执行时序后，在PushT中程时域提升11.3个百分点，最长时域提升14.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 时间抽象可以改善冻结的紧凑LeWM，但前提是高层搜索必须与低层控制器保持兼容。

Abstract: We investigate whether temporal hierarchy can improve LeWorldModel on long-horizon goal-conditioned control. We introduce Hi-LeWM, an extension that freezes the pretrained low-level LeWM and adds high-level planning over latent subgoals. We evaluate Hi-LeWM on PushT and Cube across increasing goal offsets. Hierarchy does not automatically improve performance: at short horizons, the best configuration uses a one-step high-level horizon, while longer horizons reveal a mismatch between the learned high-level action space and the inference-time search distribution. Experiments with true future latent subgoals show that the frozen low-level controller can execute well-aligned intermediate targets, indicating that high-level subgoal generation is the main bottleneck. Unconstrained search can select latent macro-actions that appear favorable under the learned model but produce poor control targets. Constraining search around macro-actions encoded from training trajectories, with appropriate subgoal execution timing, recovers useful hierarchical regimes, improving over flat LeWM by +11.3 percentage points at medium-range horizons and +14.7 percentage points at the longest PushT horizon. Overall, temporal abstraction can benefit compact frozen LeWM, but only when high-level search remains compatible with the low-level controller

</details>


### [77] [TrustVLA: Mechanism-Guided Inference-Time Defense Against Vision-Language-Action Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12571)
*Pinhan Fu,Xianda Guo,Xuetao Li,Wenke Huang,Ruilin Wang,Weiheng Zhao,Wei Sui,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型的后门攻击存在一个共同的内在机制——紧凑因果足迹(compact causal footprint)，即攻击触发区域在注意力上被聚焦、空间上紧凑且具有因果性。基于此提出TrustVLA防御方法，通过监测逐token逐层 epistemic 不确定性，实现无需重训练的触发检测、定位和观测恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉或语言防御方法很少解释触发后的VLA表征特征，也无法在不重新训练的情况下恢复行为，存在防御空白。

Method: 识别出两种独立设计的VLA后门攻击(BadVLA和INFUSE)共有的紧凑因果足迹机制；基于此提出TrustVLA，将可信分类中的Dirichlet证据框架适配到VLA策略中，监测逐token逐层的认知不确定性，通过反事实机制分数下降定位紧凑支持区域，并利用局部修复恢复观测。

Result: 在OpenVLA/LIBERO和π0.5迁移评估中，TrustVLA降低攻击成功率，同时保持干净任务性能。

Conclusion: TrustVLA提供了一种无需重训练、机制引导的视觉触发VLA后门防御方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are deployed through pipelines that end users cannot audit, and a poisoned VLA can behave normally on clean observations while a small visual trigger redirects a long-horizon robot policy before any failure becomes observable. Existing vision or language defenses rarely explain what a triggered VLA representation looks like or how to recover behavior without retraining. We study this gap through two independently proposed VLA attacks from groups with distinct injection strategies, BadVLA and INFUSE; the latter persists after downstream clean adaptation. Across the evaluated poisoned models, we identify a recurring internal mechanism: a \emph{compact causal footprint}, namely a small visual support that is attention-seeded, spatially compact, and \emph{causal} in a precise sense -- masking it returns a clean-calibrated evidence-evolution score to the normal operating region. This footprint motivates TrustVLA, a mechanism-guided inference-time defense that adapts the Dirichlet evidence framework from trusted classification to monitor per-token, per-layer epistemic uncertainty in VLA policies. With only a small clean calibration set, TrustVLA (i)~detects abnormal evidence evolution, (ii)~localizes the compact support by counterfactual mechanism-score drop, and (iii)~recovers the observation by localized inpainting. Across OpenVLA/LIBERO and $π_{0.5}$ transfer evaluations, TrustVLA reduces attack success while preserving clean-task performance, providing a retraining-free, mechanism-guided defense for visual-triggered VLA backdoors.

</details>


### [78] [Streamlining stereo differentiable rendering for marker-free real-time tracking of surgical robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12604)
*Yanghe Hao,Martin Huber,Christos Bergeles,Tom Vercauteren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于立体可微渲染的无标记手术机器人实时位姿跟踪方法，通过序列优化与CUDA并行加速，在1080p分辨率下实现30fps实时跟踪，精度达1.7cm/0.6deg（静态）和1.2cm平均3D误差（动态），显著优于FoundationPose基线，在遮挡场景下表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于标记的手术机器人跟踪在杂乱手术室内容易受到遮挡，影响可靠性与安全性。无标记方法可减少设置时间、提升安全性并支持多机器人协作，但现有无标记方法在实时性、精度和遮挡鲁棒性方面仍有不足。

Method: (i) 序列优化：跨帧传播位姿估计，配合运动自适应超参数调优；(ii) CUDA流并行化：将分割与优化任务并行处理，结合CUDA图加速分割，提升整体推理速度。

Result: 30fps实时1080p跟踪（原始roboreg仅14fps）；静态精度1.7cm/0.6deg，动态27,460帧平均3D误差1.2cm，遮挡帧平均误差1.53cm；相比FoundationPose：动态估计提升11%（遮挡下提升63%），静态估计提升250%，推理速度快6倍。

Conclusion: 立体可微渲染能够在高分辨率下实现实时的无标记手术机器人跟踪，性能与有标记方法相当，并显著超越基础模型基线，为手术机器人安全、高效跟踪提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Purpose: Marker-based tracking of surgical robots is occlusion-prone in cluttered operating rooms. We evaluate stereo differentiable rendering for marker-free, real-time robot pose tracking, potentially improving safety, reducing setup time, and enabling multi-robot interaction. Methods: We extend the markerless pose estimation framework roboreg to online dynamic tracking via (i) sequential optimisation that propagates pose estimates across frames with motion-adaptive hyperparameter tuning, and (ii) CUDA stream parallelisation of segmentation and optimisation, combined with CUDA-graph accelerated segmentation. We evaluate on 38 unobstructed and 5 occluded displacement sequences with static start/end ground-truth calibrations and dynamic marker-based reference tracking. Results: We achieve real-time 1080p tracking at 30 fps (up from 14 fps for vanilla roboreg), matching the camera frame rate. Accuracy reaches 1.7 cm / 0.6 deg against static ground truth and 1.2 cm mean 3D error over 27,460 frames against the marker-based reference (1.53 cm over 1,242 occluded frames). Our method outperforms FoundationPose by 11% in dynamic estimation (63% under occlusion) and 250% in static estimation, with 6x faster inference. Conclusions: Stereo differentiable rendering enables real-time, high-resolution marker-free surgical robot tracking, on par with marker-based approaches and surpassing foundation-model baselines.

</details>


### [79] [Instance-Enriched Semantic Maps for Visual Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12630)
*Jiho Hong,Eunae Kang,Sanghyun Kim,Young-Sik Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出实例增强语义地图（Instance-Enriched Semantic Maps），通过2.5D实例级信息映射、基于LLM的鲁棒查询处理和存储高效语义表示三大贡献，在VLN任务中相比3D基线方法在目标检索和导航成功率上分别提升17%和23%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN系统缺乏实例级对象细节，对多样化用户查询的鲁棒性不足，限制了在复杂室内环境中的可靠导航。
