# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Near-Optimal Learning of Gaussian Sobolev Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11921)
*Ben Adcock,Michael Griebel,Gregor Maier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Hermite-PCA近似的全数据驱动算法，用于学习高斯Sobolev算子，该算法在近乎最优的样本复杂度下实现收敛，解决了有限正则算子的样本复杂度诅咒问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子学习中，光滑算子可被高效近似，但有限正则算子因样本复杂度诅咒只能达到次代数收敛速率。因此，亟需开发能理论上达到这些速率的算法。

Method: 采用主成分分析（PCA）和加权最小二乘方法，设计了Hermite-PCA近似算法，这是一种计算高效且谱收敛的全数据驱动方法。

Result: 提供了完整的误差分析（涵盖所有误差来源），证明了算法在Sobolev正则性越高时收敛越快（近最优），并通过数值实验验证了理论结果和算法有效性。

Conclusion: Hermite-PCA近似算法能以近乎最优的样本复杂度高效学习高斯Sobolev算子，为有限正则算子的学习提供了可行的计算方案。

Abstract: A key question in operator learning is how to design surrogate operators with provable approximation guarantees in reasonable computational time. Whereas smooth operators can be approximated efficiently, i.e., with at least algebraic convergence in the amount of training data, learning finitely regular operators is known to be less efficient. The reason is an intrinsic curse of sample complexity, which allows only subalgebraic sample complexity rates. This fact makes it all the more important to develop algorithms which provably achieve these rates. In this work, we present a fully data-driven algorithm, termed Hermite-PCA approximation, for learning Gaussian Sobolev operators with near-optimal sample complexity. It employs principal component analysis and weighted least-squares methods and is therefore computationally efficient. Moreover, it is spectral, in the sense that it achieves faster (and near-optimal) convergence the higher the Sobolev regularity. We provide a full error analysis of this algorithm, taking into account all sources of error, along with numerical experiments that verify our theoretical results and empirically confirm the efficacy of Hermite-PCA approximation for learning Sobolev operators.

</details>


### [114] [Tangent-Space Multiscale Manifold Methods for Nonlinear Elliptic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12082)
*Mats G. Larson,Anna Persson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于切空间的多尺度流形方法，用于求解非线性异质椭圆问题。该方法通过非线性重构将粗尺度状态映射到细尺度解，利用局部化非线性patch求解和单位分割进行近似，并引入切空间多尺度测试函数处理粗尺度方程。还提出了网络插值变体，用局部学习的映射近似patch输出及其切向作用。针对单调非线性扩散问题，记录了结构单调性、可微性、patch映射正则性等性质，并通过数值实验验证了缺陷的衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多尺度方法在处理非线性异质椭圆问题时存在局限性，需要一种能够有效处理非线性特征且保持粗尺度方程结构一致性的方法。

Method: 1) 从粗尺度状态通过非线性重建构造细尺度解，重建过程通过约束变分问题消除细尺度；2) 通过局部化非线性patch求解并用单位分割混合来近似理想重建映射；3) 由于近似集是非线性流形，粗尺度方程使用切空间多尺度测试函数；4) 提出网络插值变体，用局部学习映射近似patch输出和切向作用。

Result: 针对单调非线性扩散问题，建立了结构单调性、可微性、patch映射正则性和条件扰动估计，将几何稳定性机制与局部化、残差和学习缺陷分离。通过数值实验展示了缺陷的衰减。

Conclusion: 该方法为非线性异质椭圆问题提供了一种新的多尺度求解框架，理论上将定位缺陷的衰减和收敛速率分析留待后续工作，当前通过条件控制和数值验证展示了方法的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce a tangent-space multiscale manifold method for nonlinear heterogeneous elliptic problems. The method represents the fine-scale solution by a nonlinear reconstruction of a coarse state. The ideal reconstruction eliminates fine scales through a constrained variational problem, and the computable reconstruction approximates this map by localized nonlinear patch solves blended with a partition of unity. Because the approximation set is a nonlinear manifold, the coarse equation is posed with tangent multiscale test functions. We also formulate a network-interpolated variant in which only the restricted patch outputs used by the partition-of-unity blend, together with their tangent actions, are approximated by local learned maps. For heterogeneous monotone nonlinear diffusion, we record the structural monotonicity, differentiability, patch-map regularity, and conditional perturbation estimates that separate the geometric stability mechanism from localization, residual, and optional learning defects. A rigorous a priori theory for the decay of the localization defect, and the resulting convergence rates in the coarse mesh size, is deferred to a separate analysis; here these defects are controlled conditionally and their decay is demonstrated numerically.

</details>


### [115] [Broken-space Additive Schwarz Mass Inverse Approximations and (Block) Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12138)
*Oliver A. Krzysik,Ben S. Southworth,Golo A. Wimmer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种断裂空间加性 Schwarz (BRAS) 质量矩阵逆近似方法，通过应用精确的单元局部逆质量矩阵并平均回协调空间，在保持对称正定性的同时，相比对角预处理显著降低了谱条件数、Krylov 迭代次数和求解时间，在 H¹、H(curl) 和 H(div) 有限元上实现了 1.1–4.7× 的 CG 加速，并在混合 Poisson 和双调和系统上实现了 1.5–3× 的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式时间积分和基于 Schur 补的块预处理中经常需要有限元质量矩阵的求解和近似逆，而对角近似虽然廉价但在高阶元中近似效果差，需要一种更精确且计算可行的质量矩阵逆近似方法。

Method: 提出断裂空间加性 Schwarz (BRAS) 方法：在断裂有限元空间上应用精确的单元局部逆质量矩阵，然后通过局部到全局映射将结果平均回协调空间。该方法使用与标准质量矩阵组装相同的单元矩阵和映射，具有相同的单元邻接稀疏图，且对任意协调空间、网格几何和多项式基保持对称正定性。

Result: 对预处理质量矩阵证明了谱界；在二维和三维单纯形网格上的 H¹、H(curl) 和 H(div) 有限元数值实验表明，BRAS 相比对角预处理降低了谱条件数、Krylov 迭代次数和求解时间；CG 中在 p∈[1,4] 阶元上实现 1.1–4.7× 加速；在混合 Poisson 和双调和系统中实现 1.5–3× 加速。

Conclusion: BRAS 是一种比对角/Jacobi 预处理更有效的质量矩阵逆近似方法，能在不改变稀疏结构和对称正定性的前提下显著提升求解效率，适用于多种有限元类型和块预处理场景。

Abstract: Finite-element mass matrix solves and approximate inverses arise often in explicit time integration as well as Schur-complement-based block preconditioning. A diagonal approximation of the mass matrix is cheap and widely used, but can be a poor approximation, particularly for high-order elements. This paper introduces a broken-space additive Schwarz (BRAS) mass inverse approximation, formed by applying exact element-local inverse mass matrices on the broken finite-element space and averaging the result back to the conforming space. The construction uses the same element matrices and local-to-global maps as standard mass assembly, has the same element-adjacency sparsity graph as the conforming mass matrix, and is symmetric positive definite for any conforming space, mesh geometry, and polynomial basis. We prove spectral bounds for the preconditioned mass matrix, and wide-ranging numerical experiments for \(H^1\), \(H(\operatorname{curl})\), and \(H(\operatorname{div})\) finite elements on two- and three-dimensional simplicial meshes show that BRAS reduces spectral condition numbers, Krylov iterations, and solve times relative to diagonal preconditioning. For preconditioned conjugate gradient (CG), BRAS yields a 1.1--4.7$\times$ speedup over diagonal/Jacobi preconditioning across all cases tested over finite-element orders $p\in[1,4]$. Further, theory and numerical experiments show that in the block-preconditioning case, BRAS can improve Schur-complement approximations and reduce outer solve times. On mixed Poisson and biharmonic systems, BRAS yields a 1.5--3$\times$ speedup in time-to-solution over a standard diagonal-based preconditioning approach.

</details>


### [116] [Splitting Analysis for Yukawa Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12153)
*Di Fang,Jiaqi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了含Yukawa势的薛定谔方程的分裂方法，证明了多体Yukawa相互作用下算子分裂达到全局1/4阶收敛速率，并给出了对粒子数的显式多项式依赖。同时通过单体位势的一步误差下界证明该速率的不可改进性，与Coulomb势情形的最优1/4阶速率一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分裂方法是量子动力学模拟的经典工具，随着量子计算的兴起其重要性进一步提升。Yukawa势作为Coulomb相互作用的短程近似，既有物理相关性又保留了Coulomb奇异性但去除了长程尾，对其分裂方法收敛性的严格分析具有理论价值和实际意义。

Method: 理论分析上，利用显式多项式大小的Sobolev估计建立多体Yukawa系统的先验界，通过算子分裂的误差分析获得全局1/4阶收敛率；对于下界，采用Fourier分析和Kato平滑技术证明单体情形一步误差的t^{5/4}下界。

Result: 得到了多体Yukawa相互作用的全局1/4阶收敛率（对H²初始波函数），数值实验验证了理论估计；同时证明单体位势的一步误差下界为t^{5/4}，排除了任何一致全局估计优于1/4阶的可能性。

Conclusion: Yukawa势虽为短程势，但其分裂方法的最优收敛率与长程Coulomb势一致（均为1/4阶），表明该收敛阶对于含Coulomb奇性的势具有普适性。

Abstract: Splitting methods are among the most classical and fundamental tools for the simulation of quantum dynamics, and their importance has grown further with the rise of quantum computing. In this work, we analyze the Schrödinger equation with Yukawa potential, a physically relevant and widely used model potential. It may be viewed as a Coulomb interaction with exponential decay at spatial infinity, preserving the Coulomb singularity at the origin while removing the long-range Coulomb tail. We prove that the operator splitting for this unbounded Hamiltonian achieves a global $1/4$-order convergence rate in the time step for many-body Yukawa interactions, with explicit polynomial dependence on the number of particles. The result holds for all initial wavefunctions in $H^2(\mathbb R^{3N})$, the natural domain of the Hamiltonian, and our numerical experiments are consistent with the theoretical estimates. To identify the sharp obstruction behind this rate, we prove a short-time lower bound in the one-body setting of order $t^{5/4}$ for the one-step error, which rules out any uniform global estimate of order better than $1/4$ in general. This agreement with the optimal $1/4$ rate in the Coulomb case is particularly interesting, as Yukawa potential is short-ranged compared to Coulomb potential. For the many-body upper bound, one of the new technical ingredients is the explicit polynomial-in-system-size Sobolev estimates of many-body Yukawa systems. These estimates are crucial for obtaining fully a priori bounds that depend only on the norms of the initial states, rather than on the solution at time $t$. For the one-body lower bound, we leverage a new analysis argument based on Fourier analysis and Kato smoothing.

</details>


### [117] [A Log-Gaussian Scale-Space Limiter for Hybrid Continuum--Ballistic Gas Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12218)
*Bjørn Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于混合连续-弹道气体动力学的对数高斯尺度空间限制器，通过在布斯数对数空间中构造高斯累积概率作为连续和弹道权重，实现了Navier-Stokes-Fourier通量与动力学/自由分子通量之间的平滑数值过渡，并在DVM/BGK傅里叶和库埃特基准测试中通过参数校准将平均剖面误差降低了约40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合连续-弹道气体动力学问题中，连续介质区域（Navier-Stokes-Fourier）与稀薄/自由分子区域之间的数值过渡需要平滑且渐近正确的处理方法，现有方法在连续和自由分子极限下存在修正分支失效的问题。

Method: 将对数Knudsen数空间中的高斯累积概率定义为互补的连续权重和弹道权重，以此构造限制器；将Navier-Stokes-Fourier通量与半范围Maxwellian动力学通量混合，形成守恒型有限体积界面通量。

Result: 在一维DVM/BGK傅里叶和库埃特基准测试中，稀薄修正改善了宏观剖面；通过参数扫描确定了校准参数K0=0.03和sigma=2.5，组合平均剖面误差降低了约40%。

Conclusion: 该工作是概念验证性的数值方法与校准研究，证明了对数高斯尺度空间限制器在混合气体动力学中的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a log-Gaussian scale-space limiter for hybrid continuum-ballistic gas dynamics. The method defines complementary continuum and ballistic weights as Gaussian cumulative probabilities in logarithmic Knudsen-number space. This construction gives a smooth numerical transition between Navier-Stokes-Fourier and kinetic/free-molecular fluxes and suppresses asymptotically invalid correction branches in the continuum and free-molecular limits. The limiter is incorporated into a conservative finite-volume interface flux by blending a Navier-Stokes-Fourier flux with a half-range Maxwellian kinetic flux. Numerical tests using one-dimensional DVM/BGK Fourier and Couette benchmarks show that rarefaction corrections improve macroscopic profiles relative to NSF. A parameter scan over the transition center and width gives a DVM/BGK-calibrated pair K0=0.03 and sigma=2.5, reducing the combined mean profile error by about 40% for the tested planar wall-bounded flows. The paper is intended as a proof-of-concept numerical method and calibration study.

</details>


### [118] [Contour-count indicator fields for visible pole clusters in meromorphic continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12256)
*Zhiliang Deng,Xiaomei Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对从圆形边界数据向外亚纯延拓中的可见极点簇问题，提出了一种基于等高线计数的指示器方法，通过聚合经验行列式零点信息构建标量指示场，结合固定超水平集和零维持续同源作为后处理，实现极点簇的可视化与认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纯有限极点模型下，行列式特征可以因式分解为反映外部极点倒数的多项式；然而当存在全纯背景、有限采样和噪声时，单个行列式的根不稳定，难以直接用于极点恢复。因此需要一种鲁棒的聚合方法来识别可见的极点簇。

Method: 从正傅里叶系数构建的行列式特征出发，在倒数极点平面上定义标量指示场：每个采样点的场值记录在以该点为中心的小等高线内恰好包含一个经验行列式零点的行列式阶数和移位所占比例。固定超水平集提取可见极点簇，零维持续同源作为阈值鲁棒的后处理步骤。

Result: 建立了纯极点等高线计数、Rouché稳定性、指示场对比度、固定阈值分量恢复以及持续间隙稳定性之间的确定性理论联系。孤立且具有足够留数和间距的极点产生稳定的高值分量，而弱极点、邻近极点、边界附近极点或噪声主导的极点则产生低值、短寿命或合并的分量。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种簇认证与成像方法，而非无条件的全极点恢复算法，适用于从带有噪声的有限采样边界数据中识别可靠的极点结构。

Abstract: We develop a contour-count indicator method for visible pole clusters in outward meromorphic continuation from circular boundary data. The method starts from determinant characteristics built from positive Fourier coefficients. In the pure finite-pole model, the correct determinant characteristic factors into a polynomial whose zeros are the reciprocals of the exterior poles. In the presence of a holomorphic background, finite sampling, and noise, roots of individual determinants are unstable and are used only as local evidence. We aggregate this evidence into a scalar indicator field on the reciprocal pole plane: at each sampling point, the field records the fraction of determinant orders and shifts for which a small contour centered at that point encloses exactly one empirical determinant zero. The resulting field plays the role of a sampling-type imaging functional for pole visibility. Fixed superlevel sets give visible-pole clusters, while zero-dimensional persistent homology is used only as a threshold-robust post-processing step. We prove deterministic results linking pure-pole contour counts, Rouché stability, indicator-field contrast, fixed-threshold component recovery, and persistence-gap stability. These results explain why isolated poles with sufficient residue and separation generate stable high-value components, whereas weak, close, boundary-near, or noise-dominated poles may give low, short-lived, or merged components. The framework is a cluster-certification and imaging method, not an unconditional all-pole recovery procedure.

</details>


### [119] [A time-decoupling scheme for mixed-dimensional poroelastic models with fractures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12309)
*Dongchun Tang,Linshuang He,Shuyu Sun,Minfu Feng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对含裂缝混合维孔隙弹性模型的锁自由解耦方法。通过引入总压力将裂缝Biot系统重构为四场公式，开发了时间解耦格式，采用Taylor-Hood元离散位移与总压力、Lagrange有限元离散压力，理论证明了能量稳定性和误差估计，并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决含裂缝混合维孔隙弹性模型中传统耦合方法计算成本高、参数限制严格以及锁死现象的问题。

Method: 引入总压力将裂缝Biot系统重构为位移-总压力-基质压力-裂缝压力的四场公式；初始步采用全耦合，后续步先解流动问题再解力学问题的时间解耦方案；力学方程中加入稳定项以放松参数限制；空间上位移/总压力用Taylor-Hood元，压力用Lagrange元。

Result: 建立了连续模型的能量耗散律；半离散和全离散格式的能量稳定性；全离散格式的误差估计；证明了方法的锁自由特性；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提解耦方法在保证能量稳定性和精度的同时避免了锁死现象，降低了参数限制，为含裂缝孔隙弹性问题提供了高效可靠的数值求解方案。

Abstract: We propose a locking-free decoupling method for a mixed-dimensional poroelasticity model with fractures. By introducing the total pressure, the fractured Biot system is reformulated as a four-field formulation involving the displacement, total pressure, matrix pressure, and fracture pressure. We establish an energy dissipation law for the continuous model, which shows its consistency with the second law of thermodynamics. Based on this formulation, a time-decoupled scheme is developed. At the initial time step, a fully coupled scheme is employed, while for subsequent time steps, the flow problem is solved first, followed by the mechanics problem. A stabilization term is incorporated into the mechanical equation to help reduce the restrictions imposed on the model parameters in the stability analysis. Energy stability is established for the semi-discrete scheme. For the spatial discretization, the displacement and total pressure are approximated by the Taylor--Hood element, while Lagrange finite elements are used for the matrix and fracture pressures. A fully discrete decoupled scheme is then constructed. Energy stability and error estimates are derived for the fully discrete scheme, and the method is shown to be locking-free. Numerical experiments are presented to support the theoretical results.

</details>


### [120] [Diffusive method for multiple class data classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12313)
*Toyohiko Aiki,Honoka Yano,Takeshi Ohtsuka*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于热方程无限传播速度和强最大值原理的多类数据分类算法，通过比较热方程解的符号差异在极短时间间隔内实现分类，并给出了高维特征空间中离散训练数据的数值算法，在 handwritten digits 数据库上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用热方程的物理特性（无限传播速度和强最大值原理）来设计一种简单且有效的多类数据分类方法，避免传统分类算法中复杂的优化过程。

Method: 以训练数据集的示性函数作为热方程的初始条件，求解热方程；通过比较两个解在极短时间间隔内的符号差异来建立分类准则；针对高维特征空间中的离散训练数据设计了数值算法。

Result: 在手写数字识别数据库上进行了数值实验，验证了所提方法的分类性能。

Conclusion: 基于热方程的无限传播速度和强最大值原理的分类算法是有效的，能够简洁地处理多类数据分类问题。

Abstract: A simple algorithm for multiple class data classification is proposed, which is based on the infinit speed of propagation and the strong maximum principle for the heat equation. In this method, solutions of the heat equation whose initial data are the characteristic function of the training dataset are considered. The classification is established by the sign of the difference of two solutions in a very short time interval. A numerical algorithm for discrete training data in high-dimensional feature space is proposed. As an application of the proposed method, numerical results on the classification of handwritten digits database are presented in this paper.

</details>


### [121] [Residual-Certified Adaptive Tracking of Solution Manifolds in Parametric Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12351)
*Yiran Xing,Yuandi Xu,Sulei Hu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种残差认证的自适应方法，通过结合局部POD降阶、全物理残差检查、状态距离快照遗忘、高保真重采样和轻量级物理信息神经校正，在参数化动力系统中跟踪局部解流形。算法维护当前活跃局部分支，在残差指示失效时更新，并在折叠邻域等退化区域执行更严格的局部更新。数值实验验证了该方法在低维动力学、非线性非局部残差补偿和近折叠模型失效情况下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局参数-状态映射学习方法在复杂参数化动力系统中难以兼顾计算效率与物理一致性，尤其当解流形存在多分支、折叠等退化结构时，全局模型容易失效且缺乏可解释性。需要一种自适应方法，能在不同参数区域动态切换局部模型，确保物理一致性并高效分配计算资源。

Method: 提出残差认证的自适应局部模型管理方法：(1) 基于局部POD（本征正交分解）构建降阶代理模型；(2) 使用全物理残差检查作为模型有效性认证机制；(3) 采用状态距离驱动的快照遗忘策略，控制局部模型覆盖范围；(4) 在残差超标时触发高保真重采样，更新局部分支；(5) 引入轻量级物理信息神经校正（Physics-Informed Neural Correction）补偿非线性非局部残差；(6) 在正则分支上通过残差-误差控制确定阈值，在折叠等退化区域采取更严格的局部更新策略。

Result: 在Ostwald熟化（低维动力学）、粒子群体平衡模型（非线性非局部残差补偿）和Bratu方程（近折叠模型失效）三个数值测试中，所提方法均有效：能在困难参数区域集中高保真计算，保持代理预测与物理一致性之间的可解释联系，并实现活跃分支的可靠跟踪。

Conclusion: 残差认证的局部模型管理策略能够在参数化动力系统中实现可解释的自适应计算，通过在困难区域集中高保真资源、在简单区域使用低成本代理，兼顾了效率、精度和物理一致性。该方法为多分支解流形的在线跟踪提供了系统框架。

Abstract: This paper presents a residual-certified adaptive method for tracking local solution manifolds in parametric dynamical systems. The method combines local POD reduction, full physical residual checks, state-distance snapshot forgetting, high-fidelity resampling, and a lightweight physics-informed neural correction. Instead of learning one global parameter-to-state map, the algorithm maintains the currently active local branch and updates it when the residual indicates loss of validity. The analysis explains why residual thresholds are meaningful on regular branches through local residual-error control, and why stricter local updates are needed near folds or other degenerate neighborhoods. Numerical studies on Ostwald ripening, a particle population-balance model, and the Bratu equation test the approach across low-dimensional dynamics, nonlinear nonlocal residual compensation, and near-fold model failure. The results show that residual-certified local model management can concentrate high-fidelity computation in difficult parameter regions while preserving an interpretable link between surrogate prediction, physical consistency, and active-branch tracking.

</details>


### [122] [An Adaptive Fourier Spectral Method for the Vlasov-Poisson system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12368)
*Seung Yeon Cho,Giovanni Russo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态自适应傅里叶谱方法，结合高阶时间分裂，通过自适应补零扩展波数域来避免相空间丝化引起的混叠，无需人工滤波即可精确保持质量和L²模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准谱方法依赖人工滤波抑制相空间丝化误差，但会随时间退化物理解释的精度和结构，亟需一种无需人工平滑的自适应方法。

Method: 采用动态自适应傅里叶谱方法，结合高阶时间分裂；通过谱尾监测检测新出现的精细结构，动态扩展波数域（自适应补零），实现精确三角插值。

Result: 在朗道阻尼和多种不稳定性问题的数值实验中验证了该方案的鲁棒性，能够保持质量并在动态分辨尺度内有效维持L²模。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了传统滤波器的人工平滑，在抑制丝化混叠的同时不牺牲物理结构的准确性，为Vlasov-Poisson系统数值模拟提供了一种高保真解决方案。

Abstract: Numerical simulation of the Vlasov-Poisson system faces fundamental challenges due to phase-space filamentation. Standard spectral methods rely on artificial filtering to suppress errors, which inadvertently degrades physical structures and accuracy over time. This paper proposes a dynamically adaptive Fourier spectral method combined with high-order time splitting to overcome these limitations. To prevent filamentation-induced aliasing, we dynamically expand the wavenumber domain via adaptive zero-padding whenever spectral tail monitoring detects emerging fine-scale structures. Acting as an exact trigonometric interpolation, it inherently avoids the artificial smoothing of traditional filters, preserving mass and effectively maintaining the $L^2$-norm within the dynamically resolved scales. Numerical experiments on Landau damping and various instability problems validate the robustness of our scheme.

</details>


### [123] [SinCoTrap: A High-Order Locally Corrected Trapezoidal Rule for Periodic Singular Integrals in Arbitrary Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12390)
*Hengzhun Chen,Yingzhou Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: SinCoTrap 是一种针对周期性奇异积分的高阶局部校正梯形法则，通过解析延拓广义黎曼ζ函数获得显式校正权重，在均匀张量网格上仅修改少量近奇异点的权值即可实现 O(h^{2p+2+d-s}) 的误差率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性奇异积分（核函数 |x|^{-s}, 0<s<d）在科学计算中广泛出现，但传统梯形法则收敛缓慢，现有修正方法或依赖于网格细化、或需要非均匀网格，缺乏一种即保持均匀张量网格结构又具备高阶精度的普适性方法。

Method: 基于局部校正梯形法则，在保持均匀张量网格的前提下，仅对奇异点附近固定小模板内的权值进行修正；通过解析延拓技术将校正权重转化为广义黎曼ζ函数的特殊形式，得到与网格无关的显式解析公式，可针对每组 (d,s,p) 预计算并查表使用。

Result: 对于校正阶数 p，SinCoTrap 达到 O(h^{2p+2+d-s}) 的渐近误差率；校正权重可通过快速计算的闭式公式获得，无需每层网格重新求解线性系统。

Conclusion: SinCoTrap 提供了一种兼具计算效率与高阶鲁棒性的通用框架，适用于任意维度 d 和参数 s 的周期性奇异积分，在保持网格简单性的同时实现了可预测的高阶精度。

Abstract: We present SinCoTrap (Singularity-Corrected Trapezoidal Rule), a high-order locally corrected trapezoidal method for periodic singular integrals in arbitrary dimension $d$ with kernel $|\boldsymbol{x}|^{-s}$, $0<s<d$. The scheme preserves the uniform tensor grid and modifies only a fixed, small stencil of weights near the singularity. For a correction order $p$, the resulting quadrature attains the error rate $O(h^{2p+2+d-s})$. We derive explicit, mesh-independent limiting correction weights via analytic continuation of a special generalization of the Riemann zeta function, yielding rapidly computable formulas that can be pretabulated for each $(d,s,p)$. This makes SinCoTrap both efficient in application and robust for high-order accuracy across a broad class of periodic singular integrals.

</details>


### [124] [A Fourier-Aware Projection-Based Periodic Parareal Method for Time-Periodic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12402)
*Chenyi Tan,Yuncheng Xu,Yehao Zhang,Yangfeng Su*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对时间周期性问题，提出了基于傅里叶感知投影空间的投影校正方法加速周期平行时间算法(PP-PC)，并给出收敛性分析，实验表明比Krylov增强方法更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间周期性问题广泛存在于工程与科学计算中，但现有周期平行时间算法(PP-PC)及其投影校正方法收敛速度有限，需要进一步加速。

Method: 提出傅里叶感知的投影空间构造方法，结合新的校正方案；建立基于残差校正的投影PP-PC收敛分析框架；导出局部单步收敛估计，利用时间傅里叶分解将未解析误差由尾部泄漏量控制。

Result: 线性与非线性问题的数值实验表明，傅里叶感知PP-PC所需外迭代次数少于Krylov增强PP-PC；线性问题误差追踪尾部泄漏界；非线性问题量化了局部估计中的各贡献项。

Conclusion: 傅里叶感知投影空间能有效加速投影PP-PC收敛，理论界与实际衰减一致，算法具有良好的实用前景。

Abstract: Time-periodic problems arise when the desired solution is a periodic steady state rather than a transient trajectory. The periodic parareal algorithm with a periodic coarse problem (PP-PC) is a periodicity-preserving parallel-in-time approach for such problems. Projection-based correction can accelerate convergence of both parareal and PP-PC. In this paper, we propose a Fourier-aware construction of projection spaces and a new correction scheme to further accelerate the convergence of projection-based PP-PC. We develop a convergence analysis of projection-based PP-PC with the discrepancy-based correction scheme for general nonlinear time-periodic problems. For an arbitrary orthogonal projection, we derive a local one-step convergence estimate controlled by the unresolved error and explicit nonlinear contributions. A temporal Fourier decomposition bounds the unresolved error by a tail-leak quantity, which is small when dominant error modes are selected and their coefficients are captured by the projection space. For linear problems, the nonlinear contributions vanish, yielding a globally valid one-step tail-leak convergence estimate under weaker assumptions. Experiments on linear and nonlinear problems show that Fourier-aware PP-PC requires fewer outer iterations than Krylov-enhanced PP-PC. For the linear problems, the errors track the tail-leak bound. For the nonlinear problems, the experiments quantify the unresolved-error and explicit nonlinear contributions in the local one-step estimate and show that the evaluated tail-leak estimate follows the observed decay.

</details>


### [125] [Efficient Path Reconstruction in Prehistoric Human Migration: An Adaptive Dijkstra's Algorithm Based on Wavelet Compression for Topographic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12408)
*Max Brockmann,Lena Perlberg,Angela Kunoth*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应小波方法，通过动态压缩地形数据，在保持关键路径拓扑的同时显著加速Dijkstra算法，用于 prehistoric 迁徙路线重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于高分辨率DEM的最小成本路径分析（LCPA）在大规模考古建模中计算密集，Dijkstra等算法速度过慢，亟需一种在不损失关键路径拓扑的前提下加速计算的方法。

Method: 采用自适应小波方法对地形数据进行动态压缩：在地形复杂区域（如山口）保留高分辨率，在均匀区域进行平滑降采样，从而加速Dijkstra算法的执行。

Result: 在迁徙场景下验证了该方法的有效性，显著提升了计算速度，同时保留了关键的路径拓扑信息。

Conclusion: 自适应小波压缩方法能够在加速大规模考古路径计算的同时不损失路径拓扑的准确性，为 prehistoric 迁徙路线重建提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The reconstruction of prehistoric migration routes requires the accurate evaluation of effective distances across complex topographies. Traditional Least-Cost Path Analyses (LCPA) using high-resolution elevation models often lead to dense computational grids, making algorithms like Dijkstra's prohibitively slow for large-scale archaeological modelling. In this paper, we present a novel approach using adaptive wavelet methods to dynamically compress topographic data. By retaining high resolution only in areas of high topographic complexity (e.g., mountain passes) and smoothing homogeneous regions, we significantly accelerate the Dijkstra algorithm without losing essential routing topology. We demonstrate the efficacy of this method using migration scenarios.

</details>


### [126] [Strong order one-half convergence of a coupled tamed Euler--Peano scheme for reflected stochastic differential equations with super-linearly growing coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12415)
*Ziheng Chen,Caiyun Hua,Meng Cai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对带反射的随机微分方程，在无界凸区域且漂移与扩散系数超线性增长条件下，提出了一种耦合驯化Euler-Peano格式，证明了状态过程与边界调节器的1/2阶强收敛性，并通过随机Ginzburg-Landau型系统的数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射SDE的数值方法大多处理Lipschitz系数或线性增长条件，而实际应用（如随机Ginzburg-Landau系统）中的系数往往超线性增长，且反射区域可能无界，这给数值逼近带来挑战。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1. 建立耦合单调性条件和多项式局部Lipschitz假设下反射SDE的适定性及解的矩有界性。2. 提出耦合驯化Euler-Peano格式：漂移项和扩散系数平方项用共同因子驯化，再通过Skorokhod问题修正路径，从而保持漂移-扩散的强制结构并保证数值解的一致矩估计。

Result: 证明了受约束状态过程与边界调节器的强收敛阶均为1/2，恢复了标准Euler型格式在反射框架下的强收敛阶。数值实验支持理论收敛速率并验证了格式保持约束边界的能力。

Conclusion: 该耦合驯化Euler-Peano方案有效处理了超线性增长系数与无界凸反射区域的双重困难，为更广泛的反射SDE数值分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study strong numerical approximations for reflected stochastic differential equations in possibly unbounded convex domains with super-linearly growing drift and diffusion coefficients. Under a coupled monotonicity condition and polynomial local Lipschitz assumptions, we first establish the well-posedness of the reflected SDE and derive uniform moment bounds for its solution. We then introduce a coupled tamed Euler--Peano scheme, in which the drift and the squared diffusion coefficient are tamed by a common factor and the resulting Euler--Peano path is corrected through the Skorokhod problem. This common taming factor preserves the drift--diffusion coercivity structure and yields uniform moment estimates for the numerical solution. We prove strong convergence of order $1/2$ for both the constrained state process and the boundary regulator, thereby recovering the standard Euler-type strong order in this reflected setting. Numerical experiments for a reflected stochastic Ginzburg--Landau type system illustrate the constraint preservation of the scheme and support the theoretical convergence rate.

</details>


### [127] [A mixed precision algorithm for the matrix square root](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12430)
*Bowen Gao,Daniel Kressner,Meiyue Shao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合精度算法用于计算矩阵平方根，该算法结合低精度的Schur分解和高精度的近似牛顿迭代细化，可在温和条件下恢复全工作精度，并在x86-64架构上相比固定精度Schur算法减少执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用日益强大的低精度硬件提升线性代数求解器性能，同时通过迭代细化等方法恢复工作精度，填补混合精度算法在矩阵平方根计算方面的空白。

Method: 将低精度下的Schur分解方法与通过近似牛顿法执行的迭代细化相结合，形成混合精度算法。

Result: 在x86-64架构上的数值实验表明，该算法相比固定工作精度的Schur算法，能够频繁地减少执行时间。

Conclusion: 对于对称正定矩阵，在温和条件下可恢复全工作精度；所提算法在实际架构上具有性能优势。

Abstract: Mixed precision algorithms can significantly enhance the performance of linear algebra solvers by leveraging increasingly powerful low precision hardware while recovering working precision accuracy through, for example, iterative refinement. In this paper, we propose a novel mixed precision algorithm for computing matrix square roots. Our algorithm combines a Schur decomposition approach in low precision with iterative refinement performed through an approximate Newton method. We perform a detailed convergence analysis of the approximate Newton method. For the special case of symmetric positive definite matrices, this analysis implies that one can recover full working precision accuracy under mild conditions. Numerical experiments on x86-64 architectures indicate that our algorithm frequently reduces execution time compared with a fixed working-precision Schur algorithm.

</details>


### [128] [Continuous Cross Approximation of Matrices Arising Out of Kernel Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12540)
*Sumit Singh,Sivaram Ambikasaran*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于残差能量的连续核矩阵低秩近似框架，通过自适应选择最优节点最小化残差能量，实现类似连续版自适应交叉逼近（ACA）的方法，理论证明了收敛性和误差特征，实验表明其逼近精度接近截断SVD且对离散化稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩近似方法（如ACA）主要针对离散核矩阵设计，缺乏连续视角下的理论保证和自适应节点选择策略。本文旨在建立连续核函数的残差能量驱动低秩近似框架，使其在理论上更完备且在实践中更鲁棒。

Method: 方法基于连续核函数，通过自适应选取最优节点（optimal nodes）以最小化每步残差能量，构建一系列秩-1更新；该过程被解释为连续版本的ACA。理论分析表明残差核仍属紧算子类，逼近误差由残差能量精确刻画。

Result: 数值实验显示，该方法在多种核函数上的逼近误差接近截断奇异值分解（SVD）；对采样和离散化方式具有强鲁棒性；连续残差能量与离散逼近误差高度一致，验证了方法的稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提方法作为经典交叉逼近技术的连续对应物，兼具理论严格性与实际有效性，为连续核矩阵的低秩近似提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We propose a residual energy-based framework for constructing low-rank approximations of kernel matrices arising from continuous kernel functions. The method operates in a continuous setting and is based on the adaptive selection of pivot nodes, referred to as \emph{optimal nodes}, which are chosen to minimize the residual energy at each step. This leads to a sequence of rank-$1$ updates of the residual kernel and admits a natural interpretation as a continuous analog of Adaptive Cross Approximation (ACA). From a theoretical perspective, we show that the residual kernels remain in the class of compact operators and that the approximation error is exactly characterized by the residual energy. We provide convergence guarantees showing that the method yields monotonic error reduction under an alignment condition and achieves geometric decay under practically motivated assumptions. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves approximation errors close to those of the truncated singular value decomposition across a range of kernel functions. The method exhibits strong robustness with respect to sampling and maintains stable performance across different discretizations. Furthermore, the close agreement between the continuous residual energy and the discrete approximation error highlights the consistency of the formulation. These results establish the proposed approach as a theoretically grounded, practically effective continuous counterpart to classical cross-approximation techniques.

</details>


### [129] [Deep Learning-based Surrogate Modelling of the LOD Method for Multiscale Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12570)
*Marc Haltmayer,Jaemin Seo,Yuseung Lee,Sungyeop Lee,Jaehoon Jeong,Jae Yong Lee*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对多尺度椭圆PDE问题，本文提出结合局部正交分解（LOD）方法与神经算子的混合框架LOD-MSNO，以克服传统数值方法的计算瓶颈和现有神经算子对强异质/振荡系数精度不足的缺陷，在保持计算效率的同时提升求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度问题（如材料科学、流体动力学、气候系统等）中，传统数值方法需要极细网格才能精确解析精细尺度特征，计算成本过高；而现有神经算子模型在强异质或振荡系数场景下精度不足。

Method: 提出LOD-MSNO混合方法：（1）利用LOD方法将解表示为问题自适应基函数的线性组合，作为强多尺度先验；（2）通过数据驱动的算子学习解决LOD的主要计算瓶颈；（3）为系数学习框架提供理论误差估计。

Result: LOD-MSNO在具有挑战性的多尺度输入上，精度优于现有神经算子基线，同时基本保留了神经算子的计算效率。

Conclusion: LOD-MSNO结合LOD的多尺度先验与神经算子的数据驱动效率，为求解含粗糙高对比度系数的椭圆PDE提供了一种准确且高效的新范式。

Abstract: Multiscale problems are notoriously difficult to tackle using traditional numerical methods, as accurately resolving fine-scale features often requires prohibitively fine discretizations. This challenge is particularly pronounced in applications such as materials science, fluid dynamics, climate systems, chemical processes, and complex networks. Recent neural operator models provide a promising data-driven alternative, but frequently struggle to achieve sufficient accuracy in the presence of strongly heterogeneous or oscillatory coefficients. In this work, we focus on the solution of elliptic PDEs with rough and high-contrast inputs. The Localized Orthogonal Decomposition (LOD) method is a well-established numerical approach for such problems, but it comes, however, at a substantial computational cost. We investigate the performance of popular neural operator architectures on these challenging multiscale problems and identify key limitations in their ability to resolve fine-scale structure. To overcome these challenges, we introduce LOD-MSNO (LOD-Multiscale Neural Operator), a hybrid approach that leverages the LOD method as a strong multiscale prior by building on its representation of the solution as a linear combination of problem-adapted basis functions, while addressing its main computational bottlenecks through data-driven operator learning. We further provide theoretical error estimates for the proposed coefficient-learning framework. Lastly, we demonstrate the potential of our proposed method to outperform current neural operator baselines in terms of accuracy for challenging multiscale inputs, while mainly retaining the computational efficiency of neural operator models.

</details>


### [130] [Tensor-Based Reduced-Order Modeling for Optimization-Based Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12613)
*Sahidul Islam,Andreas Mang,Maxim Olshanskii*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了张量降阶建模（TROM）框架，用于解决基于优化的参数依赖动力系统反问题。该方法通过张量列格式直接逼近参数到观测的映射，利用低秩张量结构加速计算并改进反问题求解，在两个非平凡反问题中验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数依赖动力系统的反问题通常计算成本高昂，尤其是在高维参数空间和非凸优化场景下。现有降阶建模方法多聚焦于正问题加速，而将低秩张量结构系统性地融入反问题求解流程（包括目标函数构建、高斯-牛顿量计算、优化初始化等）的框架尚不充分。

Method: (1) 采用TT-SVD或TT-Cross压缩技术将参数到观测的映射近似为张量列车格式；(2) 将低秩张量表示嵌入正则化非线性最小二乘反问题； (3) 在降阶坐标中重写反问题，无需构建完整观测空间雅可比矩阵即可组装高斯-牛顿量；(4) 在离散参数网格上进行基于TROM的目标最小化，可作为独立近似优化或后续高斯-牛顿求解的数据驱动初始化。

Result: 在两类反问题（异质介质导热反问题、FitzHugh-Nagumo参数估计）中验证了方法性能。数值实验系统评估了ROM近似误差、测量噪声、正则化、初始化策略、空间离散化及参数维数增加的影响。结果表明TROM能以显著降低的在线计算代价复现全阶反演行为，且在更高维、含噪、强非凸条件下具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TROM框架为基于优化的反问题提供了一种高效且鲁棒的求解路径。降阶坐标反演、基于张量的优化以及适当的正则化三者结合，能有效提升在高维、噪声干扰和强非凸环境下的反问题求解性能。

Abstract: We develop a tensor reduced-order modeling (TROM) framework for optimization-based inverse problems governed by parameter-dependent dynamical systems. The approach approximates the parameter-to-observation map directly in tensor-train format, using either TT-SVD or TT-Cross compression, and integrates the resulting representation into a regularized nonlinear least-squares formulation. Beyond accelerating forward evaluations, the low-rank tensor structure is used to reformulate the inverse problem in reduced coordinates, assemble the Gauss--Newton quantities without forming the full observation-space Jacobian, and perform TROM-based objective minimization over the discrete parameter grid. This tensor optimization step can be used either as a stand-alone approximate minimization procedure or as a data-informed initialization for a subsequent Gauss--Newton solve. The method is studied for two inverse problems: an inverse heat-transfer problem in a heterogeneous medium, where the unknown parameters describe the locations of multiple low-conductivity inclusions, and a FitzHugh--Nagumo parameter-estimation problem with a highly nonconvex optimization landscape. Numerical experiments assess the effects of ROM approximation error, measurement noise, regularization, initialization, spatial discretization, and increasing parameter dimension. The results show that TROM can reproduce the behavior of full-order inversion at a substantially reduced online cost. The experiments also demonstrate that reduced-coordinate inversion, tensor-based optimization, and appropriate regularization improve robustness in higher-dimensional, noisy, and strongly nonconvex regimes.

</details>


### [131] [Contour integral methods and structured perturbations for linear differential-algebraic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12628)
*Nicola Guglielmi,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将轮廓积分方法推广到受代数约束的线性动力系统的时间积分中，通过拉普拉斯变换和逆变换的数值求积求解微分代数方程，并针对参数化DAE提出基于伪谱计算的积分轮廓调优策略，实现了对参数化解族的高效逼近和模型降阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Runge-Kutta格式在处理微分代数方程时会出现精度阶数下降的问题，因此需要一种高效且精确的替代方案来处理带有代数约束的线性动力系统时间积分问题。

Method: 首先将拉普拉斯变换应用于线性微分代数方程的柯西问题，通过数值求积规则逼近拉普拉斯逆变换来重构时域解；然后针对线性参数化DAE，利用结构-非结构伪谱计算来调优CIM框架中的积分轮廓，使得单个积分轮廓能逼近整个参数化解族。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性，并支持了理论分析结论。

Conclusion: 所提出的广义CIM框架为DAE时间积分提供了高效精确的替代方案，且通过轮廓调优策略可有效支持参数化问题的模型降阶。

Abstract: We generalize the contour integral methods (CIM) framework to the time integration of linear dynamical systems that are subject to algebraic constraints at all times during their evolution. The proposed approach relies on applying the Laplace transform to the Cauchy problem associated with a linear system of differential-algebraic equations (DAE), and subsequently reconstructing the time-domain solution by approximating the inverse Laplace transform via a suitable quadrature rule. This procedure yields an efficient and accurate alternative to classical Runge-Kutta schemes, which are well known to exhibit order reduction in accuracy when applied to DAE.
  In the second part of the paper, we address linear parametric DAE and propose an efficient strategy for tuning the integration contour in the CIM framework using suitable structured-unstructured pseudospectral computations. This allows the identification of a single integration profile capable of approximating an entire family of parametric solutions, thereby facilitating the efficient application of model order reduction techniques.
  Finally, numerical experiments are presented to validate the proposed methodology and support the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [132] [Hermite spectral approximation for functions with endpoint singularities using exponential transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12648)
*Haiyong Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有端点奇异性的函数，引入基于指数变换（单指数SE、双指数DE、误差函数EF）的Hermite谱逼近方法，分析了无缩放和有缩放两种情况下的收敛性，推导了最优缩放因子，并与sinc方法进行了数值比较，还讨论了在求积和求根算法中的扩展应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有端点奇异性的函数在使用Hermite谱逼近时的收敛速度问题，通过指数变换和缩放策略提升逼近效率。

Method: 采用单指数（SE）、双指数（DE）和误差函数（EF）三种指数变换将端点奇异性问题转化为适合Hermite谱逼近的形式；分别分析无缩放和有缩放情况下的收敛率，推导最优缩放因子；通过数值实验与sinc方法进行对比。

Result: 无缩放时方法以根指数速率收敛；有缩放时收敛速度显著提升，且Hermite方法在与sinc方法使用相同项数时具有相当或更优的精度。

Conclusion: Hermite谱逼近结合指数变换和最优缩放可有效处理端点奇异函数，在精度上媲美甚至优于sinc方法，并可推广至求积和求根算法。

Abstract: In this paper we introduce Hermite spectral approximation for functions with endpoint singularities using exponential transforms, including single exponential (SE), double exponential (DE) and error function (EF) transforms, and present a comprehensive convergence analysis for these approximations without and with scaling. In the case without scaling, we show that these methods converge at some root-exponential rate. In the case with scaling, we derive optimal scaling factors for each of exponential transforms and show that the convergence rate of Hermite spectral approximation can be significantly improved. Numerical comparisons with sinc method are present and it is shown that Hermite method has comparable or superior accuracy performance when using the same number of terms. Extensions to quadrature and rootfinding algorithm are also discussed.

</details>


### [133] [Mass-Conserving Saddle Dynamics via Generalized Inner Product: Theory, Algorithms, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12715)
*Longjing Li,Guanghua Ji,Zhen Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一形式的鞍点动力学框架，揭示了不同内积选择对质量约束泛函鞍点计算的影响，建立了鞍点与线性稳定稳态的等价关系，并通过数值实验验证了H^{-1}和L^2内积下的收敛阶，最后应用于相场模型发现新的鞍点及其连接性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不同内积选择对质量约束泛函鞍点动力学的影响，填补保守系统中内积如何影响鞍点计算和求解景观的理论空白。

Method: 提出统一形式的鞍点动力学框架，建立鞍点与线性稳定稳态的等价性；在离散H^{-1}和L^2内积下推导具体动力学形式，并数值验证收敛阶；应用于具有驱动力的相场模型，分别采用Neumann和周期边界条件。

Result: 发现了先前未报道的鞍点及其连接性，证实了内积选择丰富了保守系统的解景观。

Conclusion: 内积选择对保守系统中鞍点结构具有显著影响，不同内积可揭示更丰富的解景观和鞍点连通性。

Abstract: To reveal the effect of the inner product choice, we present a unified formulation of saddle dynamics for the functional F with a mass constraint under different inner products. We establish the equivalence between the index-k saddle points and the linearly stable steady states of the corresponding dynamics. Further, we present the dynamics with discrete H^{-1} and L^2 inner products and numerically verify the convergence orders of both dynamics. Finally, we apply the method to a phase field model with driving force under Neumann and periodic boundary conditions. The results uncover previously unreported saddle points and their connectivity, highlighting how the choice of inner product enriches the solution landscape in conservative systems.

</details>


### [134] [A Mesh-Free Solver for Multi-Phase Mullins-Sekerka Flow: Triple Junctions and Ninety-Degree Boundary Contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12759)
*Tokuhiro Eto*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种无体网格的数值求解器，用于处理二维空间和半平面内多相Mullins-Sekerka流问题，采用电荷模拟方法表示化学势，通过零空间投影实现区域面积精确守恒，并验证了三同心圆精确解下的收敛性、计算代价和条件数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相Mullins-Sekerka流动涉及界面由曲率驱动并通过调和化学势场耦合，传统方法需要体网格或奇异积分处理，计算复杂。本文旨在开发一种无体网格、高精度且结构保持的数值方法。

Method: 使用电荷模拟方法（基本解法的一种变体），每个化学势由曲线外电荷点上的基本解表示，避免体网格和奇异积分。半平面边界上的无通量条件通过镜像电荷精确施加。通过零空间投影离散面积约束，在拓扑事件前实现有界相面积在速度层面的机器精度守恒。

Result: 在三同心圆精确解上验证了方法的收敛性、计算代价和条件数表现良好。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效处理多相Mullins-Sekerka流问题，包括三相点处的曲线网络和半平面边界上的正交接触，且结构保持特性确保了面积守恒精度。

Abstract: A bulk mesh-free solver for the multi-phase Mullins--Sekerka flow in $\mathbb R^2$ and in a half-plane bounded by a Neumann wall is developed. In the underlying mathematical model, interfaces driven by their curvature are coupled through a harmonic chemical-potential field. We use a charge simulation method, a variant of the method of fundamental solutions: each chemical potential is represented by fundamental solutions centered at charge points off the curve, so no bulk mesh or singular integral is required. It treats curve networks separating several phases at triple junctions, including phases that occupy more than one region; on the half-plane boundary the no-flux condition is imposed exactly by image charges, and mobile contacts stay orthogonal to the wall. The discretization is structure-preserving: between topological events, every bounded phase area is conserved to machine precision at the velocity level by a null-space projection of the discrete area constraints. The junction connectivity is fixed, the only topological event being the disappearance of a region enclosed by a single closed curve and incident to no junction. The proposed scheme is validated against an exact three-concentric-circle solution in terms of convergence, cost, and conditioning.

</details>


### [135] [A Least Squares Weak Galerkin Finite Element Method for Fokker-Planck Type Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12803)
*Chunmei Wang,Shangyou Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Fokker-Planck型二阶椭圆方程的最小二乘弱Galerkin有限元方法，利用最小二乘公式得到对称正定离散系统，并建立了唯一性和最优阶误差估计的理论基础，数值实验验证了方法的鲁棒性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非光滑扩散张量带来的数值挑战，为Fokker-Planck型方程设计稳定、高效的数值方法。

Method: 采用最小二乘公式构造对称正定离散系统，利用弱Galerkin框架中局部构造的弱二阶偏导数和弱散度设计数值格式。

Result: 建立了离散解的唯一性和离散能量范数下的最优阶误差估计，数值实验验证了理论结果和方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的LS-WG方法能有效处理非光滑扩散张量，具有对称正定性和最优收敛性，是求解Fokker-Planck型方程的一种可靠数值方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a least squares weak Galerkin (LS-WG) finite element method for a class of second order elliptic equations of Fokker-Planck type. To address the numerical challenges arising from non-smooth diffusion tensors, the proposed method utilizes a least-squares formulation that yields a symmetric positive definite (SPD) discrete system. The numerical scheme is designed by employing locally constructed weak second order partial derivatives and the weak divergence commonly used within the weak Galerkin framework. A rigorous theoretical foundation is provided, establishing the uniqueness of the discrete solution and deriving optimal-order error estimates in a discrete energy norm. Finally, extensive numerical experiments are reported to validate the theoretical findings and demonstrate the robustness and performance of the numerical scheme.

</details>


### [136] [Low-Precision Rank Compensation for Matrices and Tensor Trains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12969)
*Ibrahim El Mountasser,Khalide Jbilou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出秩补偿策略：将低精度存储节省的内存用于提高近似秩，以抵消精度降低带来的扰动。在矩阵和Tensor-Train分解上验证了该方法的有效性，实现了精度不降反升或持平、内存大幅节省和加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低数值精度虽能减少存储和内存流量，但会提高扰动下限。作者希望利用节省下来的内存来增加近似秩，从而补偿精度损失，实现精度与存储效率的双赢。

Method: 1. 矩阵：基于奇异值误差恒等式，给出可直接检验的充分条件——要求新增奇异分量能抵消低精度存储引入的扰动。2. Tensor-Train：基于截断增益测量和舍入核心扰动，给出条件性后验扩展。实验涉及FP32/FP64/FP16混合精度设置。

Result: 矩阵：100个截断主导配置全部认证为误差不增且精度严格提升，平均误差比0.963，存储比为58.8%(FP32)和29.4%(FP16)。FP16仅在尾秩压力测试中失效。加速方面：最大批次A100加速比1.28×(FP32)和2.12×(FP16)。TT：合成测试FP32/FP16分别在10/20和14/20中取得精度-内存双赢；真实张量测试分别为44/60和54/60；重建加速比1.38×(FP32)和1.94×(FP16)。

Conclusion: 秩补偿策略能有效使低精度近似在多数情况下达到不差于甚至优于高精度基线的精度，同时大幅节省存储并带来实际加速。该方法在矩阵和TT分解上均得到验证，失败案例集中于接近扰动下限的尾秩压力场景。

Abstract: Lower numerical precision reduces storage and memory traffic but raises the perturbation floor. We study rank compensation: reinvesting saved memory in a larger approximation rank. For matrices, the singular-value error identity yields a directly testable sufficient condition requiring the additional singular component to offset the perturbation from storing the rank-augmented approximation in lower precision. On ten SuiteSparse matrices, all 100 truncation-dominated configurations (50 FP32 and 50 FP16) are certified non-increases and strict accuracy wins, with mean error ratio $0.963$ and storage ratios $58.8\%$ and $29.4\%$ relative to the FP64 baseline. FP16 failures occur only in tail-rank stress tests near the perturbation floor. At the largest resident matrix-application batch, compensated FP32 and FP16 achieve geometric-mean A100 speedups of $1.28\times$ and $2.12\times$; neither accelerates the smallest batch. For Tensor-Train (TT) approximation, we give a conditional a posteriori extension based on the measured truncation gain and rounded-core perturbation. Across three-way and six-way synthetic tests, FP32 and FP16 achieve combined accuracy-memory wins in 10 of 20 and 14 of 20 trials. On public hyperspectral tensors and FROSTT top-active subtensors, the corresponding counts are 44 of 60 and 54 of 60; four FP16 Salinas-A tail-stress cases fail. No certified TT case exceeds the FP64 error beyond numerical tolerance. Reconstruction of six public tensors yields geometric-mean compensated speedups of $1.38\times$ (FP32) and $1.94\times$ (FP16). Timings cover resident downstream kernels, not factorization, transfers, or end-to-end acceleration.

</details>


### [137] [A posteriori error analysis for the Navier-Stokes equations with non-smooth data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13014)
*María Gabriela Armentano,Mauricio Mendiluce*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有L2 Dirichlet边界数据的平稳Navier-Stokes方程，提出了一种正则化公式以克服解的正则性不足问题，采用Taylor-Hood P2P1单元构建了基于残差的后验误差估计器，并在数据小性假设下证明了该估计量的可靠性和有效性，为低正则性Dirichlet边界数据下不可压缩流的自适应有限元方法提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准后验误差估计技术无法直接应用于具有L2 Dirichlet边界数据的平稳Navier-Stokes方程，因为解的正则性不足，因此需要引入正则化方法以建立适定的近似问题。

Method: 引入正则化公式得到原始问题的适定近似，采用Taylor-Hood P2P1单元进行协调有限元离散，构建基于残差的后验误差估计器，并在数据小性假设下推导可计算的上下界以关联估计量与精确解和有限元近似之间的误差。

Result: 证明了所构建的后验误差估计器在合适范数下是可靠且有效的，即估计量能够准确反映有限元误差，并给出了可计算的上下界。

Conclusion: 为低正则性Dirichlet边界数据下不可压缩流的自适应有限元方法的分析和实现提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the stationary Navier-Stokes equations with Dirichlet boundary data in L2, a setting in which the limited regularity of the solution prevents the direct application of standard a posteriori error estimation techniques. To address this issue, we introduce a regularized formulation that yields a well-posed approximation of the original problem and admits a conforming finite element discretization. Using Taylor-Hood P2P1 elements, we construct a residual-based a posteriori error estimator and establish its reliability and efficiency under suitable smallness assumptions on the data. We derive computable upper and lower bounds in an appropriate norm that relate the estimator to the error between the exact solution of the original Navier-Stokes problem and its finite element approximation, showing that the estimator accurately reflects the finite element error. These results provide a rigorous foundation for the analysis and implementation of adaptive finite element methods for incompressible flows with low-regularity Dirichlet boundary data.

</details>
